### [1] [Improving On-board Shape Models Using Sun Sensors and Implicit Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24976)
*Zacharia A. Rudge,Giacomo Acciarini,Dario Izzo,Dominik Dold,Moritz Fieback,Said Hamdioui*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅利用低成本太阳传感器掩星数据和粗略先验知识，端到端生成并优化不规则小天体（如小行星）星载3D形状模型的新方法，适用于资源受限的立方星任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带宽受限、计算资源有限的小型航天器（如CubeSat）难以使用传统成像或激光雷达进行高精度小天体建模的问题，亟需一种低开销、高鲁棒性的自主3D建模方法。

Method: 提出EclipseNets——一种可微分的掩星边界神经模型，首先从轨道运行中的实时进出掩星事件中学习并优化2D轮廓；再以这些轮廓为监督，驱动一个具周期性激活的紧凑神经符号距离场（SDF），实现隐式几何表示的增量式高保真重建。

Result: 在Eros小行星的真实轨道动力学仿真中，轮廓交并比（IoU）从0.70提升至0.87，并生成了结构一致、能表征细部与凹陷的3D重建结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为资源受限航天器提供了可行的自主小天体三维建模能力，有望集成于未来小天体探测的自主任务架构中。

Abstract: We propose an original, end-to-end approach to generate and refine the onboard 3D shape model of irregular small bodies, such as asteroids, using only low-cost Sun sensor eclipse data and coarse a priori knowledge, tailored for resource-constrained missions like CubeSats. Leveraging EclipseNets, a differentiable neural model of eclipse boundaries, our pipeline first refines 2D silhouettes by learning from real-time entry and exit events during spacecraft orbits, avoiding the limitations of traditional imaging or lidar in bandwidth-starved environments. These silhouettes then supervise a compact neural signed-distance field with periodic activations, enabling incremental updates to a high-fidelity implicit geometry representation capable of capturing fine-scale features and concavities. In simulations of the body Eros under realistic orbital dynamics, the method increases silhouette IoU from 0.70 to 0.87 and produces coherent 3D reconstructions, suggesting our approach could find a place in future autonomous architectures for small-body exploration.

</details>


### [2] [EuclidFM: Foundation Model for Compact Photometric Survey Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25080)
*Reyhaneh Vojoudi Salmani,Mahsa Berahman,Saeed Ansarifard,Michael M. Fausnaugh,Seyed Mohammad Sadegh Movahed*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了EuclidFM，一个基于自监督学习的紧凑型模型，利用欧几里得巡天快速数据发布1（QDR1）的异构测光数据，通过掩码自动编码器Transformer学习源天体的对象级表征。该模型在三个深场90,258个源上训练，生成128维嵌入，有效编码形态与测光信息，并在VIS星等预测和不确定性建模中展现优越性能；但其在类星体识别任务中的泛化能力尚待验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对欧几里得巡天中不同天体测光覆盖不均、缺乏统一表征的问题，亟需一种能从异构测光数据中自监督学习鲁棒对象级嵌入的方法，以支持下游多样的天文学分析任务。

Method: 采用掩码自动编码器（MAE）架构的Transformer模型，在Euclid QDR1三个深场共90,258个天体的多波段测光通量及不确定性数据上进行自监督预训练：随机遮蔽部分波段输入，重建被遮蔽的通量与不确定性，从而学习得到每个源的128维嵌入向量。

Result: 嵌入能有效区分点源与展源（近邻一致性显著高于随机）；冻结嵌入后线性模型预测VIS星等达0.954–0.980相关性；对低不确定性（≤1 μJy）源，嵌入使log不确定性预测RMSE降低≥24%；分类器识别出1150个类星体候选体，但仅21/156具外部证认的候选体显示AGN证据。

Conclusion: EuclidFM成功构建了适用于异构测光数据的通用天体表征，显著提升星等与不确定性预测性能，具备广泛下游应用潜力；但其在高纯度类星体识别等特定任务上的迁移能力与可靠性仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: We present EuclidFM, a compact self-supervised model that learns object-level representations from heterogeneous catalogue photometry in Euclid Quick Data Release 1. Using 90,258 sources across three Euclid Deep Fields, a masked-autoencoder Transformer reconstructs withheld fluxes and uncertainties from available measurements, producing a 128-dimensional embedding per source. The embeddings encode morphological and photometric information: sources with high point-like probability have, on average, 12.4 similarly classified neighbours among their 15 nearest neighbours, compared with 4.3 under field- and magnitude-stratified randomization. Linear models trained on frozen embeddings predict VIS magnitudes with correlations of 0.954-0.980 across three held-out fields. For sources with VIS aperture-flux uncertainties up to 1 microJy, incorporating embeddings reduces the root-mean-square error in predicted log uncertainty by at least 24% relative to a magnitude-only model. A classifier trained on spectroscopically confirmed sources identifies 1,150 quasar candidates, but only 21 of the 156 candidates with accepted external counterparts show AGN evidence, including 12 spectroscopic quasars. The classifier's transferability to the wider photometric catalogue therefore remains uncertain, and its purity and completeness require further evaluation. Independently of classification, EuclidFM provides a common representation for downstream survey analyses when photometric coverage differs between sources. Code and data are available at https://github.com/AI-Driven-HEP/EuclidFM.

</details>

*M. Mars,S. Y. Haffert,J. R. Males,L. M. Close,K. Van Gorkom,O. Guyon,A. D. Hedglen,P. T. Johnson,M. Y. Kautz,J. K. Kueny,R. Landman,J. Liberman,J. D. Long,M. Lucas,J. Lumbres,E. A. McEwen,A. McLeod,T. Nguyen,L. A. Pearce,L. Schatz,E. Tonucci,K. Twitchell,A. J. Weinberger*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向MagAO-X系统的、完全物理可微的焦面低阶波前传感器（FLOWFS）数字孪生模型，用于高精度非线性波前重建与高速线性化闭环控制，显著降低残余低阶像差和抖动导致的恒星杂散光，在实验室和实测中均验证了其高精度、可迁移性和实时控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残余低阶像差和自适应光学后端的指向抖动是小内工作角日冕仪中恒星杂散光的主要来源，限制了高对比度成像性能。

Method: 构建基于物理的FLOWFS光学模型，并利用实验室与实测数据进行校准；利用该校准模型实现非线性相位恢复、推导模型驱动的线性重构器，并生成实时控制所需的参考图像。

Result: 校准后的数字孪生模型准确复现传感器响应并恢复主导低阶像差；实验室中非线性重建残余约6–7 nm（线性为~11 nm），且框架可跨不同日冕仪与波长配置迁移；实测中仅需少量重校准即可逼近大气残余极限，并支持kHz级稳定闭环控制。

Conclusion: 完全物理的数字孪生模型可在单一可解释框架下统一实现高精度低阶波前重建、高速线性化控制，并支持从实验室到实测、跨观测配置的无缝迁移。

Abstract: Context. Residual low-order aberrations and post-AO tip-tilt jitter are major drivers of stellar leakage in small-inner-working-angle coronagraphs and limit high-contrast imaging performance. Aims. The aim of this work is to develop a fully physical and differentiable digital twin for the focal plane low-order wavefront sensor on MagAO-X. This digital twin can be used to provide accurate non-linear and fast linearised wavefront sensing and control to deal with low-order aberrations. Methods. We build a physics-based optical model of the focal plane low order wavefront sensor (FLOWFS) system and calibrate it using laboratory and on-sky measurements. The calibrated model is used for non-linear phase retrieval, deriving a model-based linear reconstructor, and generating the corresponding reference image for real-time control. Results. The calibrated digital twin accurately reproduces the sensor response and recovers the dominant low-order aberrations. In the laboratory, non-linear reconstruction achieves residual floors of about 6 to 7 nm over the pupil, compared with about 11 nm for the linear reconstructor, and the same digital-twin framework transfers across different coronagraph and wavelength configurations. On-sky, the model can be recalibrated with limited changes, achieves reconstruction performance close to the residual atmospheric floor, and provides a linear reconstructor that enables stable closed-loop low-order control at kHz speed. Conclusions. A fully physical digital twin can unify accurate low-order wavefront reconstruction, fast linearised control, and transfer across observing configurations and from lab to on-sky operation within a single interpretable framework.

</details>


### [4] [SlotFlow-GB: A Neural Framework for Trans-Dimensional Inference of Overlapping Galactic Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26057)
*Niklas Houba,Giovanni Giarda,Davide Piras*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出SlotFlow-GB神经框架，用于LISA任务中从强重叠信号中快速、联合推断银河系双星（GBs）的数量与参数，无需预设源数目，实现毫秒级推断并支持后续精确似然估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA将观测到大量强重叠的银河系双星信号，传统方法需预先设定源数目且计算昂贵，难以应对高密度、高重叠场景下的跨维贝叶斯推断问题。

Method: 提出SlotFlow-GB——一种基于可学习流模型（normalizing flow）与slot attention机制的神经框架，直接建模跨维后验分布，输出源数目概率及各源参数密度，端到端完成推断。

Result: 在10源/2μHz频带的LISA仿真数据上，94%情况下源数目估计误差≤1；能准确识别强频域重叠的个体源；单频带推理耗时仅41ms（GPU）或530ms（8线程CPU）；输出分布可有效初始化后续似然优化。

Conclusion: SlotFlow-GB为大规模、强重叠引力波源提取提供了高效、可扩展、可解释的深度生成式解决方案， bridging amortized inference and precise parameter estimation for LISA data analysis.

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe the millihertz gravitational-wave sky, including millions of long-lived Galactic binaries (GBs), tens of thousands of which are expected to be individually resolvable throughout the mission's lifetime. Extracting these sources requires jointly determining their number and physical parameters from strongly overlapping signals, posing a computationally demanding trans-dimensional inference problem. We introduce SlotFlow-GB, a neural framework that constructs a learned approximation to the trans-dimensional posterior, producing a probability distribution over source multiplicity and parameter densities for individual binaries in a single evaluation, without requiring their number to be specified in advance. We demonstrate this framework using year-long LISA simulations containing up to ten binaries within a 2 $μ$Hz frequency interval, focusing on a high-SNR regime in which source overlap, rather than individual-source detectability, is the primary challenge. The inferred source count differs from the true count by at most one in 94% of cases, while the model identifies individual binaries even when their signals overlap strongly in frequency. A complete evaluation, including parameter sampling, takes just 41 ms per band on one NVIDIA GH200 GPU, or 530 ms on eight laptop CPU threads. We further demonstrate that these distributions can be used to initialize subsequent likelihood-based inference, connecting fast amortized predictions with joint parameter estimation.

</details>

### [5] [Simulation-based inference for gravitational-wave cosmology: parameter reconstruction of a first-order electroweak phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25036)
*Thomas Biekötter,Andrii Dashko,Kateryna Radchenko*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于仿真的推断（SBI）方法，以高效率、高精度地从随机引力波背景（SGWB）信号中重建宇宙学一级相变模型参数，尤其适用于LISA探测强电弱相变场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测来自宇宙学一级相变的随机引力波背景（SGWB）是超出标准模型物理的明确证据，但模型参数到频谱的映射存在简并性，且强相变仅占据参数空间稀疏小区域，导致传统参数重构困难。

Method: 采用无需似然函数的仿真驱动推断（SBI）方法，首先对相变热力学参数（强度、逆持续时间、温度、泡壁速度）进行重构；随后在标准模型单态扩展框架下，对强电弱相变模型参数进行实际应用；并结合重要性采样与精确似然函数进一步校正后验分布。

Result: SBI后验分布与基于似然的MCMC结果一致，且单次数据集推断成本极低；以SBI后验为重要性采样提议分布，采样效率比先验采样高数个数量级。

Conclusion: SBI为LISA等未来引力波观测任务提供了快速、灵活且可靠的宇宙学参数重构新范式。

Abstract: The detection of a stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) from a cosmological first- order phase transition would be unambiguous evidence for physics beyond the Standard Model, with a strong electroweak phase transition (EWPT) at LISA as a prime example. Reconstructing the underlying model from such a signal is challenging, since the map from the model parameters to the spectrum is degenerate and strong transitions occupy only small and sparse regions of parameter space. We show that simulation-based inference (SBI), a likelihood-free approach applicable to generic SGWB sources, provides a reliable parameter reconstruction despite these difficulties. We first apply SBI to the thermodynamic parameters characterizing the transition, namely its strength, inverse duration, temperature and bubble wall velocity. As a concrete application, we then consider a strong EWPT in a singlet extension of the Standard Model and reconstruct the model parameters from the resulting SGWB. We find that the SBI posteriors agree with those of a likelihood-based Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis, at negligible cost per data set once the network has been trained. Using the network posterior as the proposal for importance sampling with the exact likelihood further corrects its remaining deviations, at a sampling efficiency several orders of magnitude higher than sampling from the prior. SBI thus offers a fast and flexible framework for the cosmological science case of LISA

</details>


### [6] [Revealing galaxy assembly bias with luminosity-weighted marked correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25093)
*Joaquin Armijo,Roberta Vasallo,L. Raul Abramo,Antonio D. Montero-Dorta*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光度加权标记相关函数的可控零假设检验方法，用于探测星系形成过程中除晕质量外的次级晕属性（如聚集性、年龄等）对星系分布的影响（即星系组装偏差）。通过在DESI-BGS模拟星表中注入受控的相关性，作者发现该方法能在5σ置信水平上显著区分有/无次级属性依赖的模型，证明其对隐藏在传统两点相关函数之外的次级晕信息具有高灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于晕质量的星系形成模型可能忽略次级晕属性（如晕年龄、聚集性）对星系性质的影响，即‘星系组装偏差’；而标准两点相关函数（如wp(rp)）无法分辨这种影响，亟需一种能敏感提取次级信息的新统计量。

Method: 构建两种控制模型：(A) 星系光度残差与次级晕属性s无关；(B) 光度残差与标准化次级属性s线性相关（耦合系数ρ）；二者在HOD、位置、光度函数和wp(rp)上完全一致，差异仅体现在光度标记中；定义并计算投影标记相关函数差ΔMp(rp)，理论推导其主阶正比于ρ²σ²logL ξss(rp)；在DESI-BGS模拟中进行检验。

Result: 在rp∈[0.11,1.04] h⁻¹Mpc范围内检测到显著标记聚类偏离，χ²=30.14；单参数ρ检验以>5σ（p=2.13×10⁻⁷）拒绝纯质量模型；最小可探测|ρ|≈0.52（当σlogL=0.23时）；结果对HOD设定和星系选样效应稳健。

Conclusion: 光度加权标记聚类是一种有效且灵敏的零假设检验工具，能够揭示被传统两点相关函数掩盖的、由次级晕属性驱动的星系组装偏差，为约束星系形成物理提供了新途径。

Abstract: We investigate whether luminosity-weighted marked correlation functions can reveal secondary halo information beyond that encoded by halo mass alone, as expected in scenarios of galaxy assembly bias. We propose a controlled null test in which galaxy luminosities follow the same mean luminosity--halo mass relation and scatter, while their residuals are either (A) uncorrelated with secondary halo properties or (B) correlated with a standardized secondary halo property, $s$. Both models have identical halo occupation, galaxy positions, luminosity function, and unweighted projected two-point correlation function, $w_p(r_p)$, so their distinction is encoded entirely in the luminosity marks. At leading order, the projected marked correlation function difference, $Δ\mathcal{M}_p(r_p)$, scales as $ρ^2σ_{\log L}^2ξ_{ss}(r_p)$, where $ρ$ controls the coupling between the luminosity and secondary-property residuals, $σ_{\log L}$ is the scatter in log luminosity at fixed halo mass, and $ξ_{ss}$ describes the spatial correlation of the secondary property. We test this framework using a DESI-BGS mock catalogue over $0.1<z<0.4$. For the injected dependence, we detect a marked-clustering deviation over $0.11<r_p/(h^{-1}\,{\rm Mpc})<1.04$, obtaining $χ^2=30.14$ relative to the null covariance. A one-parameter test rejects the mass-only hypothesis at greater than $5σ$ significance ($p=2.13\times10^{-7}$) for $|ρ|>0.63$. At fixed $σ_{\log L}=0.23$, correlations as weak as $|ρ|\simeq0.52$ remain detectable, with the results stable under variations in the HOD prescription and modelled galaxy selection effects. These results establish luminosity-weighted marked clustering as a sensitive null test for property-based galaxy assembly bias and secondary halo information hidden from unweighted two-point clustering.

</details>

*Charles Willard,Yakov Faerman,Samuel McCarty,Matthew McQuinn,Nir Mandelker,Thomas Quinn*

TL;DR: 本文利用AREPO代码对宇宙片状结构进行高分辨率模拟，发现冷气体在片状结构中呈现碎片化形态，主要由非线性薄壳不稳定性（NTSI）和剪切诱导涡度驱动，而非热或Richtmyer-Meshkov不稳定性；分辨率提高加剧碎片化，并显著提升(p)LLS的遮蔽分数；结果表明宇宙片可能承载大量极低金属丰度的(p)LLS，但气体性质仍未收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙片状结构中冷气体的分布与演化，特别是其在形成部分莱曼极限系统（pLLS）和莱曼极限系统（LLS）中的作用，以及相关不稳定性机制。

Method: 使用移动网格代码AREPO对红移z=3–6的单个宇宙片进行高分辨率宇宙学模拟，并对比MP-Gadget模拟结果，分析中性氢柱密度（N_H I）、碎片化形态及不稳定性机制。

Result: 发现冷气体在宇宙片中呈千秒差距尺度碎片化，由NTSI和剪切诱导涡度主导；分辨率提高使pLLS/LLS遮蔽分数增加1.5–3.5倍；宇宙片可能贡献大量超低金属丰度(p)LLS；MP-Gadget模拟显示类似碎片化但存在数值平滑；气体性质仍未收敛。

Conclusion: 宇宙片是(p)LLS，尤其是极低金属丰度系统的潜在重要场所，但当前模拟分辨率仍不足以完全收敛气体物理性质，需更高分辨率进一步研究。

Abstract: We present high-resolution cosmological simulations of individual cosmic sheets ($z=3-6$) using the moving-mesh code AREPO to study cold gas ($T\approx10^4\rm ~K$) in the diffuse cosmic web. We find cosmic sheet gas forms a fragmented morphology, evident as kiloparsec-scale variations in the neutral hydrogen column density ($N_{\rm HI}$). Fragmentation arises from the bending and disruption of the cold-dense sheet core into a multiphase medium. Nonlinear thin shell instability (NTSI) and later shear-induced vorticity drive this bending, rather than previously suggested thermal or Richtmyer-Meshkov instabilities. Higher resolution enhances fragmentation. Across a fourfold range in spatial resolution, $N_{\rm HI}$ covering fractions increase by factors of $\approx 1.5-3.5$ for partial Lyman-limit systems (pLLS) and Lyman-limit systems (LLS). An order-of-magnitude estimate suggests sheets may host a large fraction of the ultra-low metallicity (p)LLS population. Equivalent MP-Gadget simulations show qualitatively similar fragmentation, but numerical smoothing effects are present. Cosmic-sheet gas properties remain unconverged despite our high resolutions.

</details>


### [8] [First $Ω_{\rm m}-w$ Measurement Using Only Fast Radio Burst Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25312)
*Yi-Ying Wang,Shi-Jie Gao,Chi Zhang,Yi-Zhong Fan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文首次仅使用快速射电暴（FRB）数据独立约束宇宙物质密度Ω_m和暗能量状态方程参数w，基于125个定位FRB样本和新建立的色散量（DM）统计模型，得到Ω_m = 0.33^{+0.10}_{-0.11}、w = -0.80^{+0.28}_{-0.38}，并发现与Ia型超新星相反的Ω_m–w正相关简并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

The deviations are relatively small when the MG effect is weak, while they become more pronounced in models with strong deviation from GR and can exceed $15\%$ in the extreme case.

However, apparent deviation from this universality can arise if the halo mass is not defined consistently with the modified gravity model.

Abstract: We present and analyse the results of Science Data Challenge 3b (SDC3b; https://sdc3.skao.int/challenges/inference), an Epoch of Reionization (EoR) inference exercise organised by the Square Kilometre Array Observatory (SKAO) using simulated SKA data. The nine-month challenge, running from October 2024 to May 2025, represents the first community-wide comparison of EoR inference pipelines on common simulated datasets. We consider multiple datasets designed to assess performance on both foreground-free and foreground-contaminated cylindrical power spectra and imaging products, adopting different reionization scenarios and EoR simulation models. We include results from 16 teams, which employ a range of methodologies to infer the posterior distribution of the hydrogen neutral fraction across three redshift intervals spanning z=8.41 to z=6.25. Although individual methods can produce biased parameter estimates, the combined average of their estimates remains close to the input values, suggesting that independent approaches can offset method-specific biases.

Abstract: Primordial non-Gaussianity (PNG) leaves an imprint on large-scale structure through a scale-dependent galaxy bias, and properly incorporating the effects of higher order PNG into the bias expansion requires a consistent renormalization framework that extends beyond the squeezed limit of the bispectrum. We develop the multi-loop renormalization of bias operators in the presence of non-vanishing primordial n-point functions of arbitrary order, including stochastic contributions. We show that galaxy bias renormalization is determined by the multi-soft limits of primordial correlators and bias kernels. We provide explicit computations for one- and two-loop renormalization of deterministic operators up to fifth order in the bias expansion. Finally, we derive the renormalization group equations for the bias parameters at one and two loops.

Abstract: Recent cosmological results presented by the DESI collaboration show a clear deviation from the $Λ$CDM model when considering a time-dependent dark energy equation of state. This effort requires robust photometric calibration, with a particular focus on filter transmission measurement and star catalog calibration. The target for LEMAITRE is to control the calibration systematics on distance moduli below 0.4%.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用涨落流体力学方法，考虑与剪切粘滞相关的随机各向异性应力，推导出膨胀背景下的约束朗之万方程，并在弱粘滞极限下计算矢量模功率谱。

Conclusion: 确立了温通胀期间矢量扰动的新源，并提供了研究其二级效应及矢量通道大尺度白噪声的理论框架，虽无直接可观测大尺度矢量信号，但具重要理论意义。

Abstract: Vector modes are generally believed not to play a significant role in standard cosmological perturbation theory because they decay rapidly. Non-equilibrium dynamics in the radiation bath present during warm inflation can lead to shear viscous effects, requiring a description beyond the perfect-fluid approximation. We adopt the fluctuating hydrodynamic approach to account for stochastic anisotropic stress related to shear viscosity. We show that the vector mode obeys a constrained Langevin equation in an inflationary background. The resulting power spectrum, considered in the weak-viscosity regime, is proportional to the shear viscosity and it also depends on the Hubble slow-roll parameter $ε$, decaying approximately as $a^{-(1-ε)}$. This behaviour is significantly slower than the unsourced, non-viscous power spectrum, which decays as $a^{-4}$. The continuously sourced vector modes, nevertheless, decay on superhorizon scales, and the illustrative benchmark parameter choices adopted in our numerical examples give a negligible late-time vector-to-scalar ratio. Despite the absence of direct large-scale observable vector signatures, the main results establish a source of vector perturbations during warm inflation and provide a framework for studying possible secondary effects along with large-scale white noises in the vector channel.

</details>

The orbital integrations reveal a clear division of roles: \(L\) governs the compactness of the bound orbit, whereas the particle spin governs the accumulated relativistic precession. The calculation uses the geometric-optics propagation relation and includes both dispersion and finite emission duration, without a small-mass or narrow-band approximation. We then calculate how radial averaging changes the angular power for an early-produced background whose momentum channels trace a common source-fluctuation field. For a scale-invariant source spectrum, moving a thin shell leaves its angular power unchanged, so the reduction in power isolates the effect of its finite thickness. For a logarithmic momentum width of $0.5$, mass-to-momentum ratios $q=0.3,1,3$ give angular-power ratios of approximately $0.974$, $0.771$, and $0.508$ at $\ell=10$. At a representative frequency of $3\,\mathrm{nHz}$, these ratios correspond to masses of approximately $3.6\times10^{-24}$--$1.2\times10^{-23}\,\mathrm{eV}$. The exact solution of this spectral problem reveals two qualitatively different regimes. The innermost stable circular orbit and the critical impact parameter share their Schwarzschild values, together with an emitted efficiency of approximately $5.72\%$. Positive $q$ increases the local thermal emission and accelerates the accumulation of successive images around the common critical curve. Under the universal coupling assumptions associated with the quoted Solar System bounds, the fractional corrections are of order $10^{-11}$ in flux and $10^{-12}$ in temperature.

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) will observe massive black hole binaries (MBHBs) for days to months before merger, offering opportunities for advance electromagnetic follow-up. To enable such observations, inference methods must provide rapid, continuously updated, and statistically calibrated posterior estimates, while remaining robust to overlapping sources. We present TRUFFLE (Time-evolving Rapid Updates For Follow-up of LISA Events), a simulation-based inference method for MBHBs in LISA data designed to meet these requirements. Our approach uses two networks, selected according to the time to coalescence relative to the end of the analysis window, $Δt_c$. An early-warning network, operating at $Δt_c \in [4~\mathrm{h}, 2~\mathrm{d}]$, estimates the chirp mass, mass ratio, and coalescence time to support electromagnetic follow-up scheduling. A near-merger network, operating at $Δt_c \in [-1~\mathrm{h}, +4~\mathrm{h}]$, infers nine parameters, including the sky position needed for telescope pointing. Both networks process the most recent six days of data using multiband time- and frequency-domain representations, which are summarized by a transformer to condition a normalizing flow. The networks are trained on the full orbit-driven LISA response and amortized over a 30-day range of observation epochs. The near-merger posterior can be corrected through likelihood-based importance sampling, with neural inference taking seconds and likelihood reweighting approximately five minutes on a single GPU. We obtain unbiased posteriors in the presence of overlapping sources, including plunging extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, resolvable Galactic binaries, and additional MBHBs merging up to two hours before the primary merger.

A family of mixed RR, gauge, and scalar interactions exists at all orders, represented by a finite number of effective even forms. 3. The higher-derivative contact-term sector can be constructed from a finite set of basic tensor structures using polynomials in derivative symbols.

Abstract: We revisit the use of cutoff regularization in computing loop corrections to cosmological correlators. We argue that a cutoff corresponding to a fixed physical scale must be defined with respect to the local background geometry. In the presence of superhorizon perturbations, this is naturally implemented within the separate universe picture, where different local patches are regulated at the same physical scale. Expanding this cutoff in the long-wavelength background generates a series of regulator-induced contributions to loop amplitudes, which admit a diagrammatic interpretation as long-mode insertions on the corresponding loop contractions. Our cutoff prescription preserves residual spatial dilatations and volume-preserving anisotropic rescalings. It is therefore particularly relevant for loop correlators with soft external modes. We illustrate the mechanism by computing the contributions to the one-loop scalar and tensor power spectra on superhorizon scales. In both cases, the additional corrections induced by the inhomogeneous cutoff cancel the ultraviolet boundary terms arising in the ordinary diagrams. These calculations establish, within cutoff regularization, the late-time constancy of both power spectra and clarify its physical origin. We then investigate the squeezed one-loop scalar bispectrum and show that the additional cutoff contributions are essential for satisfying the consistency relation at finite cutoff. Finally, we discuss the role of the inhomogeneous cutoff in the late-time constancy of the one-loop bispectrum.

</details>

Abstract: We construct twists of spinning particles in the classical BV formalism by giving constant backgrounds to the bosonic ghosts of local worldline supersymmetry, in analogy with Costello and Li's twist of supergravity, which gives a nonzero background to the ghost of local spacetime supersymmetry. We represent the superghosts on polynomial states and gauge selected worldline R symmetries, imposing the shifted R constraints absolutely and the unshifted ones relatively. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究宇宙大尺度上是否存在宇称破缺，受四点物质关联函数分析及暴胀中动力学Chern-Simons算符可能诱导可观测宇称破缺的启发。

Method: 在包括费米子和弦论等现实UV完备模型中，分析动力学Chern-Simons项对宇称奇四谱（trispectrum）的影响，并结合当前TSS三谱观测约束进行评估。

Abstract: A question of fundamental interest is whether parity is violated on large scales in the Universe. This possibility has been motivated by recent analyses of the four-point matter correlation function. It has also been suggested that detectable parity violation could be generated during inflation by the dynamical Chern-Simons operator, a leading effective field theory correction to general relativity. In this work, we examine this possibility in realistic UV completions, including fermionic and string-theoretic examples. We show that existing bounds on the tensor-scalar-scalar (TSS) bispectrum strongly constrain the possibility of generating a detectable parity-odd trispectrum. For tensor-to-scalar ratios near current observational limits, detectability is incompatible with, or pushes against, the regime of validity of these UV completions. A detectable signal can remain possible for a sufficiently small tensor-to-scalar ratio, but only at the cost of an extremely large enhancement of the TSS vertex. Thus, while a future search for large scale parity violation is worth pursuing, generating a detectable signal within a consistent effective theory is highly nontrivial.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The modular properties of holomorphic Eisenstein series for $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ and its congruence subgroups do not carry over to their primitives. Instead, the transformation of iterated Eisenstein integrals under congruence subgroups of level $N$ introduces inhomogeneous terms comprising (among others) cyclotomic multiple zeta values of level $N$. We combine iterated Eisenstein integrals for the congruence subgroups of level $N=2$ of arbitrary iteration depth with their complex conjugates and (single-valued) level-2 cyclotomic multiple zeta values such that they define non-holomorphic modular forms. Our construction of these so-called equivariant iterated Eisenstein integrals for $Γ(2)$ and $Γ_0(2)$ proceeds at the level of generating series that take values in an infinite-dimensional (pro-unipotent) Lie group and closely follow Brown's analogous construction in the $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbb{Z})$ case. The required insertions of cyclotomic multiple zeta values are controlled by zeta generators for level 2 that normalise the corresponding (pro-nilpotent) Lie algebra of the generating series and whose Lie-bracket relations we determine systematically. Upon restriction to Eisenstein series of modular weight~2, we recover single-valued multiple polylogarithms on the genus 0 modular curve $Γ(2)\backslash \mathbb{H}$.

</details>

Abstract: Two regular black hole horizons need not have an entropy product fixed by the charges and couplings. Abstract: We study a version of the so-called D234 fermion action. On top of non-perturbative improvement to $\mathcal{O}(a)$, this action is also tree-level improved at $\mathcal{O}(a^2)$. Working in the quenched approximation, we compare this action to the clover-improved Wilson action. The simulations are performed in the isospin-symmetric, strangeness- and charm-neutral setting above the crossover temperature and at unprecedentedly large baryon chemical potentials.

Abstract: The phenomenon of confinement is arguably the key feature of gauge theories regularly studied in high-energy and condensed matter physics. This causes hadrons to exist as stable particles in quantum chromodynamics (QCD) as bound states of quarks (and gluons), and strings in pure gauge theories. The promise of realizing such strings in quantum simulator setups have fueled extensive studies of a plethora of simpler lattice gauge theories (LGTs). Quantum link models (QLMs) generalize Wilsonian LGTs and yield richer scenarios of confinement. For example, the string joining two static charges in the spin-$\frac{1}{2}$ $U(1)$ QLM fractionalize, giving rise to confining phases that break the point group and internal symmetries spontaneously. It was further shown in PRL 130, 071901 (2023) that adding annealed $Q = \pm 2$ charges to this model qualitatively changes the confinement-deconfinement transition. In this paper, we examine the finite temperature phase transition both in the presence and the absence of annealed charges through detailed extraction of critical temperature, $T_c$ , and exponents. The (effective) string tension $σ$ at zero and finite- temperatures are compared between the theory with and without annealed charges. The quantity $T_c/\sqrtσ$ for the QLM with annealed charges is similar to the usual $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory, while the theory without the charges provides a new universal value.

*William Good,Huey-Wen Lin*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于格点QCD（特别是LaMET和伪PDF方法）计算胶子部分子分布函数（PDFs）的最新进展，讨论了当前研究现状、影响、挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶子在强相互作用中起关键作用，但其在强子中的结构（尤其是胶子PDF）因实验限制而知之甚少；格点QCD近年突破使直接计算x依赖的PDF成为可能。

Method: 主要采用大动量有效理论（LaMET）和伪PDF框架，从第一性原理出发在格点QCD中计算胶子PDF。

Result: 已实现对核子及赝标量介子胶子PDF的x依赖结构的格点计算，推动了强子结构的第一性原理研究。

Conclusion: LaMET和伪PDF方法已成为格点计算胶子PDF的主流工具，但仍面临系统误差、高精度计算、夸克-胶子混合等问题，需进一步发展。

Abstract: Gluons play an essential role in the strong interaction that binds quarks into hadrons, as described by quantum chromodynamics (QCD). Understanding how the fundamental dynamics of QCD give rise to hadron structure remains one of the outstanding challenges in nuclear and particle physics. Parton distribution functions (PDFs) provide a successful framework for describing high-energy scattering processes involving hadrons. However, our knowledge of gluon PDFs remains comparatively limited because of the experimental challenges associated with directly constraining the gluon structure of hadrons. In recent years, a major breakthrough has enabled the calculation of the Bjorken-$x$ dependence of PDFs directly from lattice QCD. Among the available approaches, the large-momentum effective theory (LaMET) and pseudo-PDF frameworks have emerged as the most widely used methods and have produced the majority of lattice determinations of parton distributions. These developments have sparked rapid progress in first-principles studies of the $x$-dependent structure of hadrons, including gluon PDFs of the nucleon and pseudoscalar mesons. In this review, we summarize recent advances in lattice QCD calculations of gluon PDFs {using the LaMET and pseudo-PDF methods}, discuss the current status and impacts of these developments, and highlight the remaining challenges and future opportunities.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文应用Ruffini-Argüelles-Rueda（RAR）模型分析仙女座星系（M31）的暗物质结构，发现48–60 keV费米子质量范围可同时解释银河系与M31的中心致密质量分布，而更早热力学稳定性给出的194–387 keV范围则与M31观测矛盾，暗示其可能需引入黑洞。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协调RAR模型在银河系和M31中对中心致密暗物质核的解释，检验是否存在统一的费米子质量能同时满足两者的动力学与稳定性约束。

Result: 48–60 keV费米子质量可使RAR模型在M31中产生位于0.19 pc内的量子核以解释中心质量，且总旋转曲线统计可接受（χ²/Ndof≈1.18）；但该质量区间与最新热力学稳定性要求（194–387 keV）无交集，故若采信后者，则M31必须存在黑洞。

Conclusion: RAR模型在48–60 keV质量窗口内可统一描述银河系与M31的暗物质核，支持费米子暗物质假说；但热力学稳定性新约束与M31观测冲突，凸显模型参数张力，需进一步观测或理论修正。

Abstract: The Ruffini--Argüelles--Rueda (RAR) model describes dark matter as a self-gravitating system of massive fermions in thermodynamic equilibrium within general relativity. It suggests a dual morphology in galaxies: a highly degenerate quantum core mimicking a central supermassive black hole, and a classical halo that explains galactic rotation curves. Earlier applications of the model to the Milky Way restricted the fermion mass to $mc^{2}=48\text{--}345~\text{keV}$, and the orbits of the S-stars around Sagittarius~A$^{\ast}$ (Sgr~A$^{\ast}$) favored $mc^{2}=56~\text{keV}$, whereas a recent thermodynamic stability analysis allows only $mc^{2}\approx194\text{--}387~\text{keV}$. In this work, we apply the RAR model to the Andromeda galaxy (M31), which hosts a dark compact object of approximately $1.4\times10^{8}~M_{\odot}$ within $0.19~\text{pc}$, roughly 33 times the mass of Sgr~A$^{\ast}$. We solve the relativistic equilibrium equations for $mc^{2}=6\text{--}60~\text{keV}$. For all these masses, the fermionic core lies within $0.19~\text{pc}$ and provides the central mass without a black hole. With a bulge and disk added, the total rotation curve is statistically acceptable ($χ^{2}/N_{\text{dof}}\approx1.18$) but lies below the disk data at $3\text{--}11~\text{kpc}$, an offset attributed to the baryonic models we adopted. The Navarro--Frenk--White (NFW) and Burkert profiles fitted to the same data give a lower $χ^{2}$ but require an additional black hole. The stability of the M31 core against gravitational collapse limits the fermion mass to $mc^{2}\lesssim60~\text{keV}$. With the earlier Milky Way range, a single fermion can therefore describe both galaxies only for $mc^{2}=48\text{--}60~\text{keV}$, which includes $56~\text{keV}$. With the thermodynamic range, no fermionic core can supply the central mass of M31, so M31 would have to host a black hole.

</details>


### [94] [Eos: a merger-induced starburst at the dawn of Galactic low-$α$ disc formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25023)
*Han-Yuan Zhang,Vasily Belokurov,Sarah G. Kane,João A. S. Amarante*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析APOGEE DR17数据，识别出Eos星群——一批在Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus（GS/E）并合期间原位形成的低α、高氮弥散、晕轨道恒星；其化学与运动学特征支持其起源于并合引发的气体混合不充分的星暴，是银河系低α盘开始形成的直接观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别并确认银河系最后一次重大并合（GS/E）期间原位形成的恒星，以理解该事件如何驱动星系结构与化学演化，特别是高α盘向低α盘的过渡。

Method: 利用APOGEE DR17恒星丰度数据，结合轨道动力学（非旋转、高偏心轨道）与化学特征（低α、高[N/Fe]弥散、金属丰度梯度），筛选并表征Eos星群；对比Splash（加热盘星）与模拟（Auriga）中GS/E并合引发的星暴场景。

Result: Eos星群具有显著高于同金属丰度低α盘的氮弥散，且化学上介于GS/E吸积物质与同期原位恒星之间；其轨道越延展，化学越接近GS/E；年龄介于高α盘与低α盘之间；Auriga模拟成功复现其化学弥散、中间丰度及chemodynamical梯度。

Conclusion: Eos是GS/E并合触发的星暴遗迹，形成于两个星系间部分混合的气体‘混合通道’，标志着银河系低α盘的开端，为并合驱动的星系演化提供了首个直接观测证据。

Abstract: The last significant merger of the Milky Way, the Gaia-Sausage-Enceladus (GS/E), is thought to have reshaped the early Galaxy and to mark the transition from the high-$α$ to the low-$α$ disc. The stars formed in situ during this merger offer a direct record of that epoch but are hard to identify. Using APOGEE DR17 abundances, we study Eos, a population of in-situ low-$α$ stars on halo-like, non-rotating, eccentric orbits. The metal-poor part of Eos has a nitrogen dispersion as large as that of the pre-disc Aurora population and up to 1.7 times that of the low-$α$ disc at the same metallicity. Heating alone cannot produce this: the Splash, a disc heated onto halo orbits by the same merger and selected in the same way, shows no excess over its parent disc. This part of Eos was therefore born in poorly mixed gas. Eos is also chemically intermediate between the accreted GS/E debris and the coeval in-situ Milky Way stars, and stars with more GS/E-like chemistry move on more extended orbits. Its stars are older than the bulk of the low-$α$ disc but younger than the high-$α$ disc and the Splash. We compare these observations with a merger-induced starburst seen in an Auriga simulation. There, a GS/E-like merger funnels the stripped progenitor gas into a mixing lane of intermediate metallicity, where stars form with intermediate abundances, a large chemical dispersion and a chemodynamical gradient set by their birth site, reproducing the observed signatures of Eos. We therefore interpret Eos as the surviving relic of the GS/E merger-induced starburst, born largely from the partially mixed gas bridging the two galaxies and marking the onset of the Milky Way's low-$α$ disc. Eos thus provides a direct observational view of the star formation and galaxy evolution driven by the Milky Way's last significant merger.

</details>


### [95] [Top-heavy Initial Mass Functions are not enough at cosmic dawn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25027)
*Andrea Ferrara,Pratika Dayal*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文测试了Chon等人（2024）提出的金属丰度依赖的顶重初始质量函数（IMF）能否解释宇宙黎明时期观测到的紫外明亮星系过剩现象，发现其对净紫外光度提升有限（仅约0.3 mag），不足以单独解释该现象；但其显著增强的成对不稳定超新星（PISN）产率（比Salpeter IMF高2–6倍）可作为探测早期高质恒星种群的更优观测诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解释宇宙黎明时期观测到的紫外（UV）明亮星系异常丰富这一现象，学界提出顶重初始质量函数（IMF）假说；本文旨在检验Chon et al. (2024) 基于贫金属恒星形成模拟提出的新型金属丰度依赖顶重IMF是否能自洽地再现该观测特征。

Method: 将Chon顶重IMF与标准Salpeter IMF进行对比，自洽纳入其对紫外辐射、超新星反馈、金属增丰和尘埃生成的影响；结合DELPHI半解析模型，在红移z≈11–17、暗晕质量M_h=10^8–12 M⊙范围内计算并比较二者预测的紫外光度函数及成对不稳定超新星（PISN）产率。

Result: 在固定恒星形成率下，Chon IMF使本征紫外光度提升因子B_1500≈4.7–2.8，但因更强的超新星反馈抑制恒星形成并增加尘埃消光，净紫外提升仅B_UV≈2.0–1.06；对亮端星系（主导UV光度函数亮端者），提升更小（B_UV≈1.2–1.4，即~0.3 mag）；两IMF预测的UV光度函数差异<0.5 dex；而PISN产率差异达2–6倍。

Conclusion: 单靠顶重IMF无法解释宇宙黎明紫外明亮星系的过剩；但PISN信号差异显著，可作为探测早期高质恒星形成历史的更灵敏、更干净的观测探针。

Abstract: A top-heavy stellar initial mass function (IMF) has been proposed to explain the unexpected abundance of ultraviolet (UV)-bright galaxies at cosmic dawn. We test this possibility using the metallicity-dependent, top-heavy IMF recently proposed by Chon et al. (2024), which is motivated by simulations of metal-poor star formation. We compare this IMF with a Salpeter IMF, self-consistently including the associated changes in UV emission, supernova feedback, metal enrichment, and dust production. At fixed star formation rate, the Chon IMF enhances the intrinsic UV luminosity by a factor ${B}_{1500}\simeq4.7$-$2.8$ over halo masses $M_h=10^{8-12}\,M_\odot$. However, its larger supernova rate suppresses star formation and increases dust attenuation, reducing the net boost to ${B}_{\rm UV}\simeq2.0$-$1.06$. For halos relevant to the bright end of the UV luminosity function, the enhancement is only ${B}_{\rm UV}\simeq1.2$-$1.4$, or about $0.3$ mag. Calculations with the DELPHI semi-analytic model confirm that the self-consistent Chon and Salpeter UV luminosity functions differ by less than $0.5$ dex at $z\simeq11$-$17$. Thus, a top-heavy IMF alone cannot explain the bright galaxy excess. Conversely, the predicted pair-instability supernova abundance differs by factors of $2$-$6$ between the two IMFs, providing a potentially cleaner observational diagnostic of the high-mass stellar populations at these early epochs.

### [96] [Influence of variable IMF on the dynamical black hole mass estimates in massive ETGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25035)
*Avinash Chaturvedi,Sabine Thater,Davor Krajnović,Ignacio Martín-Navarro,Alina Boecker*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了径向变化的初始质量函数（IMF）对五个大质量早型星系中心黑洞质量动力学测量的影响，发现采用随半径变化的恒星质量-光度比（M*/L）模型会显著降低部分星系的黑洞质量估计值，表明IMF的空间变化对黑洞质量测量具有不可忽略且星系依赖性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明大质量早型星系中的恒星初始质量函数（IMF）并非普适，且存在径向变化（中心更偏重低质量恒星），这会影响恒星质量-光度比（M*/L），进而影响动力学黑洞质量估计；因此需量化该效应。

Method: 使用DYNAMITE三轴Schwarzschild轨道建模程序，结合MUSE和SINFONI积分场观测数据，对NGC3923、NGC4261、NGC4636、IC4296和IC4329五个星系进行质量建模；模型中采用由空间分辨恒星种群合成得出的径向变化M*/L（IMF自由拟合），并与恒定M*/L模型对比。

Result: 所有目标星系中心均呈现底部偏重的IMF，向外变轻；NGC3923和IC4329获得约束的黑洞质量，而其余三个仅得上限；相比恒定M*/L模型，NGC3923和IC4329的黑洞质量分别降低约83%和11%，其余星系的上限则可能升高或降低。

Conclusion: IMF和M*/L的空间变化对动力学黑洞质量测量具有非可忽略但高度星系依赖的影响，未来动力学建模必须考虑此类变化。

Abstract: Several studies have shown that the stellar initial mass function (IMF) in massive early-type galaxies (ETGs) is not universal. Recent results from stellar population synthesis models, based on integral-field unit (IFU) observations, indicate that the IMF varies radially, becoming increasingly bottom-heavy towards galaxy centres. This radial variation alters the stellar mass-to-light ratio ($\rm M_{*}/L$) and can significantly affect dynamical black hole (BH) mass estimates in massive ETGs. In this study, we investigate the impact of a radially varying IMF on BH mass estimates for five massive galaxies: NGC3923, NGC4261, NGC4636, IC4296, and IC4329. Using DYNAMITE, a triaxial Schwarzschild orbit-based modelling code, we perform mass modelling of these galaxies based on MUSE and SINFONI IFU observations. Our models incorporate radially varying stellar $\rm M_{*}/L$ profiles derived from spatially resolved stellar population modelling with a free IMF, and we compare the resulting BH masses with those obtained from dynamical models assuming a constant stellar $\rm M_{*}/L$. All galaxies in our sample show bottom-heavy IMFs in their central regions, with the IMF generally becoming lighter towards larger radii. We obtain constrained BH masses for NGC3923 and IC4329, while only upper limits are obtained for NGC4261, NGC4636, and IC4296. Compared with the constant-$\rm M_{*}/L$ models, the variable-$\rm M_{*}/L$ models yield BH masses lower by approximately 83% and 11% for NGC3923 and IC4329, respectively, while the BH mass upper limits for the other galaxies can increase or decrease. Our results demonstrate that spatial variations in the IMF and stellar $\rm M_{*}/L$ can have a non-negligible but strongly galaxy-dependent impact on dynamical BH mass measurements. These results highlight the importance of accounting for such variations in future dynamical modelling efforts.

</details>


### [97] [Twisted resonant orbits: Quantifying the deceleration of the Galactic bar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25039)
*Alice Archer,Adam Dillamore,Hanyuan Zhang,Vasily Belokurov*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: ‘V’字形结构更可能是银河系棒的动力学共振响应而非并合遗迹；该工作为测量银河系棒减速及暗物质-重子物质间动力摩擦提供了新观测探针，Gaia DR4将进一步提升精度。

Abstract: Recent structural discoveries in Gaia Data Release 3 reveal distinct, chevron-shaped over-densities in the radial phase space of the halo. Although initial interpretations identify these features as the phase-mixed relics of a past galaxy merger, their prominent asymmetry and persistence at high metallicities strongly challenge this interpretation. These features are better explained as a dynamical response to a Galactic bar, where stars are trapped in resonances. We establish the fractional $v_r$ asymmetry of the chevrons as a quantitative diagnostic tool for the bar's rotational state. We utilise test-particle simulations of a Milky Way-like galaxy to model various bar pattern speed evolutions, including decelerating and constant speed scenarios. By mapping the fractional asymmetry of the phase-space chevrons across a parameter space of bar deceleration rates and pattern speeds, we establish a direct correlation between the bar's deceleration and the rotational response of the resonant orbits. Comparing these simulated constraints with observational data from Gaia DR3 provides a framework for measuring the current deceleration of the Galactic bar, and a probe of the amount of dynamical friction occurring between the baryonic and dark matter content of the Milky Way. We find a dimensionless deceleration parameter $η\equiv - \dotΩ_b/Ω_b^2 = 0.0015^{+0.0016}_{-0.0009}$, equivalent to $\dotΩ_b = -2.23^{+1.50}_{-2.46} \, \text{km s}^{-1} \text{kpc}^{-1} \text{Gyr}^{-1}$, in agreement with previous estimates obtained with independent methods. Gaia DR4 should sharpen this measurement considerably.

</details>


### [98] [The Silk Nebula Revisited: A Comment on Wu et al. (2026) and New Constraints from Archival Survey Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26243)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验Wu等人(2026)提出的Silk Nebula可能为超新星遗迹的主张，核查其观测数据处理与解释的可靠性。
