Conclusion: Silk Nebula并非典型超新星遗迹，而更可能是Monogem Ring（距离~250–320 pc）边缘上的柱截断辐射激波结构；需远紫外与深度光学光谱、自行测量及白矮星吸收线观测进一步确认。

Abstract: Wu et al. (2026) used one LAMOST MRS-N plate to suggest that the Silk Nebula, a $17^\circ$-long \Oiii~$λ$5007 filament in Gemini, is possibly associated with a supernova remnant at a fiducial distance of $\sim$160\,pc. We test this against their published values and independent archival data. The \Ni\ airglow in their blue-arm spectrum lies at air, topocentric rest wavelengths, so their \Oiii\ velocities omit the heliocentric correction and are $10.1\pm1.0$\,\kms\ too high. Corrected and combined with DESI DR1 and SDSS spectra, \Oiii\ lies at \vhel\ $=+13.3\pm1.0$\,\kms, the velocity of \Nii\ and of the local warm ionized medium (WIM). Their \Ha\ velocities are 4.5--6.7\,\kms\ redder than \Nii. Their tabulated distances are in kpc, not units of 100\,pc, and kinematic distances carry no information in this direction. Their surface brightness exceeds independent measurements (1.4--2.5 rayleighs) by factors of 4.6--8.6. Their red-line shock criteria are met equally in their nebula-free background region and describe the WIM; their other remnant diagnostics do not constrain the filament. DESI and SDSS spectra show that the filament is Balmer-dark (\Oiii/\Hb\ $>11$) and \Oii-strong, with \ion{He}{2}/\Oiii\ $<0.026$; GALEX shows a coincident ultraviolet ridge, with no \Ha, \ion{H}{1}, dust or X-ray counterpart. These properties, and the lack of an ionizing source, exclude photoionization equilibrium and complete radiative shocks and match a column-truncated radiative shock of $\sim$80--120\,\kms\ seen edge-on, plausibly on the limb of the Monogem Ring at $\sim$250--320\,pc. Far-ultraviolet and deep optical spectroscopy, a two-epoch proper motion and high-ion absorption toward bracketing white dwarfs would be decisive.

</details>


### [99] [Ram Pressure Is Unable to Strip All Phases of the Interstellar Medium out of Spirals Falling into Galaxy Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25049)
*Guillermo Tenorio-Tagle,Sergio Martínez-González*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了星系团中螺旋星系受冲压剥离（RPS）影响的过程，重点研究湍流粘性剥离（TVS）对致密分子云的作用，并通过三维流体模拟发现TVS可在不同密度云中持续剥离气体、形成类似水母星系尾部的丝状结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冲压剥离模型难以解释致密冷分子气体的剥离机制，需深入理解云尺度上的物理过程，尤其是Kelvin-Helmholtz不稳定性驱动的湍流粘性剥离（TVS）。

Method: 采用三维流体动力学数值模拟，研究不同密度（10² 和 10³ cm⁻³）致密云在ICM中受冲压作用下的响应，重点关注TVS引发的质量损失与形态演化。

Result: TVS在两种密度云表面均持续剥离气体，形成相似的拖曳尾迹和相当的质量损失率；低密度云受冲击压缩变形强但减速小，高密度云变形弱；TVS可解释水母星系尾部中的丝状亚结构。

Conclusion: TVS是致密分子云在星系团环境中被有效剥离的关键机制，其作用独立于云密度，能自然生成观测到的复杂尾部子结构，补充并深化了经典RPS图像。

Abstract: Following the expansion of the Universe, spiral galaxies may fall at large speeds into galaxy clusters. Their interaction with the intracluster medium (ICM) may remove part of their gas and reduce their capacity for making new stars, as envisaged by Gunn & Gott. Ram pressure stripping (RPS) is commonly invoked to explain this gas loss and the wakes or tails left behind in-falling galaxies. Here we revise the impact of RPS on individual dense clouds and particularly focus on turbulent viscous stripping (TVS), driven by Kelvin-Helmholtz instabilities at the cloud-ICM interface, that affects the high-density and low-temperature molecular gas. For an extended gaseous body to decelerate, momentum from its exposed surface must be communicated into the gas by a hydrodynamic disturbance able to propagate through it. Three-dimensional hydrodynamic calculations follow this interaction with dense clouds. At cloud number densities $n_{\mathrm{cl}}=10^2$ cm$^{-3}$, ram pressure acting through the transmitted shock strongly compresses and deforms the cloud and produces only a small deceleration; at $n_{\mathrm{cl}}=10^3$ cm$^{-3}$, the slower transmitted shock produces much weaker deformation. At both cloud densities, TVS continuously removes gas from the cloud surface and forms qualitatively indistinguishable trailing wakes, with comparable mass-loss rates, suggesting that TVS acting on individual dense clouds can generate filamentary substructure within jellyfish-galaxy tails.

</details>


### [100] [Kinetic Tomography with Diffuse Interstellar Bands in APOGEE DR17](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25084)
*Philipp Frank,Andrew K. Saydjari,Catherine Zucker,Susan E. Clark,Douglas Finkbeiner,Torsten Enßlin,Andrew R. Casey,Alexandre Roman-Lopes,José G. Fernández-Trincado,Dhanesh Krishnarao,John C. Wilson*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合变分推断与高斯过程的新方法，利用APOGEE DR17中15,273 Å DIB的高质量探测数据，实现了银河系内6 kpc范围内的4D（三维位置+径向速度）气体动力学层析成像，揭示了尘埃分布与速度结构的大尺度特征（如翘曲、盘标高）及小尺度湍流性质（Kolmogorov谱斜率等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往基于弥散星际带（DIBs）的动力学层析（DIB-KT）受限于样本量小、空间分辨率粗（kpc量级），难以刻画银盘垂直结构；亟需更高精度、更大样本的4D气体分布重建方法。

Method: 融合变分推断与高斯过程，采用混合似然模型处理低信噪比探测，并对距离不确定性进行边缘化；通过模拟场和光谱注入测试校准模型，并用三维尘埃图、脉泽和年轻星团等独立示踪体验证。

Result: 获得了太阳系6 kpc内15,273 Å DIB的密度与速度三维后验分布图；测得DIB盘大尺度参数（标长、标高、翘曲）；消除了l-v图中的距离模糊；二阶速度结构函数斜率为0.64±0.04，符合Kolmogorov湍流，振幅与分子云预期一致，并初步给出压缩模与螺线模比例。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展至SDSS-V等更大DIB星表，有望在云与空洞形成及动力学研究所需尺度上解析运动学子结构。

Abstract: Context: Mapping gas flows in the interstellar medium is key to understanding the formation of structure in our Milky Way. Methods that simultaneously reconstruct clouds' distances and line-of-sight velocities are called kinetic tomography (KT). Diffuse interstellar bands (DIBs) are powerful tracers for such maps because they trace the integrated dust density and the dust-weighted velocity. Previous DIB-KT works had small samples with coarse kpc-scale resolution and could not capture vertical disk structure. Aims: Make 4D (3D position + radial velocity) maps of the Milky Way from new, high-quality 15,273 Å DIB detections in APOGEE DR17. Methods: We combine variational inference and Gaussian processes with a mixture likelihood for low signal-to-noise detections and marginalization over distance uncertainties. We calibrate the model on mock fields and spectral injection tests and validate the map against independent tracers of the dust density (3D dust maps) and velocity substructure (masers and young stellar clusters). Results: We recover a posterior distribution over 3D maps of the density and velocity structure of the 15,273 Å DIB within 6 kpc of the Sun. We measure the large-scale structure of the DIB disk (scale length, height and flare) and detect vertical distortions consistent with the warp. On smaller scales, the map resolves distance ambiguities in gas longitude-velocity diagrams. From the second-order velocity structure function, we measure a slope (0.64 $\pm$ 0.04) consistent with Kolmogorov turbulence, amplitude consistent with expectations for molecular clouds, and a preliminary ratio of compressive-to-solenoidal modes. Conclusions: With larger DIB catalogs expected from SDSS-V, this framework should resolve kinematic substructure at the scales needed to constrain the formation and dynamics of individual clouds and voids. (abridged)

</details>


### [101] [Off-Nuclear AGN Signatures in MaNGA: Spatially resolved selection using MEGACUBES](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25085)
*Sandra Jaison,Rogério Riffel,Dominika Wylezalek,Sandro B. Rembold,Sandra I. Raimundo,Rogemar A. Riffel,Marco Albán,Mark Armah,Theodor Brosi,Quinn O. Casey,Oli L. Dors,Ryan C. Hickox,Thaisa Storchi-Bergmann,Tiago H. S. Weirich*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MaNGA巡天数据，通过空间分辨光谱分析，在超过10,000个星系中识别出201个非核AGN候选体；发现其宿主星系与核AGN无显著差异，但电离气体激发态和运动学不同，且提出AGN活动具有短时（约5万年）、重复爆发的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分真正的离核AGN与由弥散电离气体等混淆源导致的假阳性信号，并探究离核AGN是否反映特殊宿主性质或局部气体动力学现象。

Method: 基于MaNGA megacubes数据，结合空间径向算法与BPT、WHaN、WHaD多维诊断图，采用改进的等效宽度和面亮度阈值分离AGN辐射与弥散电离气体。

Result: 识别出261个核AGN、201个离核AGN和159个‘宁静’复合像元主导源；核与离核AGN宿主在恒星质量、年龄、SFR、金属丰度上统计一致，但[O III]光度和[O III]/Hβ比值显著偏低；WHaN边界需从-0.4修正至-0.25以剔除大量假阳性；离核比例暗示AGN episodic寿命约5×10⁴ yr。

Conclusion: 离核AGN并非源于特殊宿主，而是低激发、未被遮蔽的真实离核电离区，支持AGN以短时、重复吸积爆发方式演化；投影效应部分但不完全解释离核现象；诊断图需针对IFU数据重新标定。

Abstract: We present a spatially resolved search for off-nuclear active galactic nucleus (AGN) candidates in the MaNGA survey. Using megacubes, we analyze over 10,000 galaxies, separating AGN activity from diffuse ionized gas with refined equivalent-width and surface-brightness thresholds. Combining a spatial radial algorithm with BPT, WHaN, and WHaD diagnostics, we classify 261 nuclear AGNs, 201 off-nuclear AGNs, and 159 'quiet' (composite-spaxel-dominated) sources. We test whether off-nuclear AGNs reside in distinct host galaxies or whether the offset is a local, gas-driven phenomenon. Nuclear and off-nuclear AGN hosts are statistically indistinguishable in stellar mass, age, star formation rate, and gas-phase metallicity. However, the excitation and kinematics of the AGN-ionized gas differ significantly: nuclear AGN spaxels have [O III]$\lambda5007$ luminosities $\sim 0.7$ dex higher than off-nuclear spaxels, whose lower [O III]/H$β$ ratios indicate genuinely lower-excitation gas rather than an obscured view of the central engine. We also find an orientation trend: 72% of nuclear hosts are face-on, versus 50% of off-nuclear hosts, so projection effects contribute to, but do not fully explain, off-nuclear detections. The classical WHaN boundary at $\log$([N II]/H$α$) = -0.4 misclassifies high-metallicity diffuse ionized gas in integral field data; an empirical shift to -0.25 removes over 48,000 spurious AGN spaxels. Finally, the off-nuclear fraction implies an episodic AGN phase lifetime of $\sim 5 \times 10^4$ yr, consistent within a factor of a few with independent fading-AGN estimates and supporting short, recurrent accretion bursts.

</details>


### [102] [A novel pathway to stellar-mass black hole growth: Re-accretion of gas from stellar wind bubbles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25110)
*D. Kománek,R. Wünsch,S. Martínez-González,S. Jiménez,D. Szécsi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过1D和3D流体模拟，研究新生恒星级黑洞在致密分子云中吸积前身星所吹出的星际气泡内气体的可能性，发现低金属丰度下直接坍缩形成的黑洞可额外吸积最多6.9倍太阳质量气体，最终质量达93倍太阳质量；而超新星爆发或高金属丰度情形下无显著吸积。该机制有助于解释引力波观测到的高质黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准恒星演化模型难以解释引力波观测到的高质恒星级黑洞的形成，本文旨在探索新生黑洞在致密分子云中通过吸积前身星产生的风泡内气体来增质的可能性。

Method: 采用1D和3D流体动力学模拟，涵盖热传导、辐射冷却、引力、电离辐射、恒星风和超新星爆发等物理过程；对500多个1D参数组合（包括云密度、束缚能、前身星质量40–257 M⊙、金属丰度0.66 Z⊙和0.016 Z⊙）进行系统研究，并用高分辨率3D模拟验证关键情形。

Result: 低金属丰度下直接坍缩形成的黑洞可从晚期慢速恒星风形成的致密内壳中吸积最多6.9 M⊙气体，最终质量达93 M⊙；而含超新星爆发或高金属丰度的情形中，快速恒星风或超新星抛射物清空气泡内部致密结构，导致无显著吸积。

Conclusion: 前身星风泡内气体再吸积是解释部分高质引力波黑洞起源的一种可行机制，但仅适用于低金属丰度且无超新星爆发的直接坍缩场景。

Abstract: The formation of high-mass stellar black holes (BHs) observed in mergers by gravitational wave observatories remains difficult to explain using standard models of stellar evolution. We investigate whether newly formed stellar-mass BHs in dense molecular clouds can significantly increase their final mass by accreting gas from pre-existing, wind-blown interstellar bubbles produced by their progenitor stars. We performed 1D and 3D hydrodynamic simulations of interstellar bubbles driven by stellar feedback, incorporating thermal conduction, radiative cooling, gravity, ionizing radiation, stellar winds, and supernova (SN) explosions. We modeled the subsequent accretion onto the central BH for two formation scenarios: core-collapse SN and direct collapse. Our parameter space study encompasses over 500 1D simulations, varying initial cloud densities, gravitational binding energies, progenitor masses ($40-257\,M_\odot$), and metallicities ($0.66\,Z_\odot$ and $0.016\,Z_\odot$), and tracks the bubble evolution for millions of years post-BH formation. Scenarios exhibiting significant re-accretion were further validated using high-resolution 3D simulations. Our simulations show that BHs formed via direct collapse from low-metallicity progenitors can accrete up to $6.9\,M_\odot$ of gas from massive inner shells formed by late-stage slow winds, resulting in final BH masses of up to $93\,M_\odot$. Conversely, we find no significant re-accretion in scenarios involving an SN explosion or high-metallicity progenitors; in these cases, fast winds or SN ejecta efficiently evacuate dense structures from the bubble interior, leaving behind a hot, low-density medium. [abridged] This mechanism may help explain the unexpectedly high masses of BHs observed in gravitational wave events.

</details>


### [103] [The HI Mass Fractions of Milky Way Analog Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25221)
*Adam Marcello,Karen L. Masters,David V. Stark*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文估计了九个银河系类似样本（MWAs）的平均HI质量分数f_HI，并发现其中大多数样本的f_HI低于银河系的最新估计值，暗示银河系可能在HI含量上具有特殊性，或当前对银河系HI含量的估计存在偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于从银河系内部观测自身存在困难，对银河系全局性质（如HI质量）的测量具有挑战性；本文旨在通过对比银河系与外部类似星系的HI质量分数，检验银河系是否在HI含量上具有特殊性。

Method: 选取九组基于不同属性与银河系相似的星系样本（MWAs），计算其平均HI质量分数f_HI = M_HI/M_star，并与银河系的最新f_HI估计值进行比较。

Result: 九个MWAs中，七个样本的平均f_HI低于银河系的最新估计值，其中两个最大样本的差异具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 银河系可能拥有异常高的HI质量分数，或者当前对其HI含量的估计偏高；该结果突显了从银河系内部进行全局测量所面临的系统性挑战。

Abstract: We estimate average HI mass fractions, f_HI = M_HI/M_star, for nine Milky Way analog samples (MWAs): galaxies selected on similarity to the MW in terms of various properties. We find most (seven) MWAs have lower <f_HI> than a recent estimate for the MW, although only the two largest have significantly lower estimates. We suggest that either the MW has an unusual f_HI for its properties, or we are overestimating its HI content, one of many challenging global measurements to make from our perspective inside our own Galaxy.

</details>


### [104] [The Cosmic Infrared Background from Population III.1 Supermassive Stars and Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25355)
*Emir Tataroglu,Jonathan C. Tan,Rosalia D. O'Brien,Devesh Nandal*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了由Pop III.1超大质量黑洞形成场景（依赖暗物质湮灭加热促成原初超大质量恒星）所预期产生的宇宙红外背景（CIB），建模了其各阶段辐射（恒星、吸积盘、星云连续谱及再分布Lyα），发现恒星贡献极小（<1%），而z~6–20期间SMBH吸积贡献更显著；最新CIB观测限制该模型的平均爱丁顿比λ_Edd ≲ 0.3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解早期宇宙中Pop III.1超大质量黑洞形成对宇宙红外背景（CIB）的贡献，以检验该模型与当前CIB观测的一致性。

Method: 基于Petkova等人（2026）的反馈调控Pop III.1模型，构建Pop III.1超大质量恒星及其伴生Pop III.2恒星的辐射模型，涵盖生长、闪致电离和早期黑洞吸积阶段，并计入恒星连续谱、吸积盘辐射、星云连续谱及中性IGM中散射再分布的Lyα辐射对CIB的贡献。

Result: Pop III.1恒星对CIB总贡献小于现有直接与弥漫CIB测量值的1%；而z~6–20期间SMBH吸积贡献在平均爱丁顿比λ_Edd ~ 0.01时即超过恒星成分；最新CIB观测将该模型的λ_Edd上限约束为≲ 0.3。

Conclusion: CIB观测可有效约束Pop III.1 SMBH形成模型中的黑洞吸积效率，表明该模型若要符合当前CIB数据，需满足较低的平均爱丁顿比。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmic infrared background (CIB) that is expected to arise from the Population (Pop) III.1 supermassive black hole (SMBH) formation scenario, which involves early SMBH seeding via supermassive primordial star formation enabled by dark matter annihilation heating. We model the emission from Pop III.1 supermassive stars, including their growth phase, flash ionization phase, and early black hole accretion phase, as well as accompanying emission from lower-mass Pop III.2 stars. The CIB is composed of stellar and SMBH accretion disk continuum radiation, nebular continuum emission, and Ly$α$ radiation redistributed through scattering in the neutral intergalactic medium. Based on the feedback regulated Pop III.1 model of Petkova et al. (2026), our fiducial model assumes $\sim 10^5\,M_\odot$ Pop III.1 stars forming by $z_{\rm flash}=20$ with a comoving number density of $\sim0.2\,{\rm cMpc}^{-3}$. For this model, the combined stellar contribution is less than $1\%$ of existing direct and diffuse CIB measurements. However, the contribution to the CIB from $z\sim6-20$ SMBH accretion is expected to be larger than the Pop III.1 stellar component for average Eddington ratios $λ_{\text{Edd}} \sim 0.01$. Latest CIB observations place constraints of $λ_{\text{Edd}} \lesssim 0.3$ for the fiducial Pop III.1 model during these epochs.

</details>


### [105] [Dark Matter Model Implications of a Candidate Sub-halo Near the Sun, and the Data Needed to Discriminate Among Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25359)
*Stefano Profumo,Sukanya Chakrabarti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了脉冲星计时加速度所暗示的太阳附近暗物质子晕的可能性，并系统分析了不同暗物质模型（如超轻波暗物质、温暗物质、暗声学振荡等）在结构形成、内部结构、存活率及辐射/散射等方面的可观测效应；同时提出了四种可打破现有观测简并性的新观测途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时观测到的局部加速度暗示太阳附近可能存在暗物质子晕，亟需厘清其对暗物质本质的约束及如何区分不同暗物质模型。

Method: 系统梳理各类暗物质模型对子晕性质的影响，并基于可观测效应（结构形成、内部结构、辐射/散射等）进行分类分析；进而提出能打破当前观测简并性的四类新观测策略。

Result: 明确了现有加速度观测局限于潮汐半径外、无法分辨内部分布、速度和子晕群体；识别出四种关键新观测路径：内部加速度测量、引力透镜与吸积观测、湮灭/衰变辐射形态分析、共动恒星群搜寻。

Conclusion: 该暗物质子晕候选体为检验多种暗物质理论提供了独特实验室；未来多信使观测（动力学、引力透镜、高能辐射、恒星天文学）协同将有望甄别其真实物理起源。

Abstract: Pulsar-timing accelerations point to a localized mass concentration near the Sun, consistent with a dark matter sub-halo. We work out what such an object implies for the nature of dark matter, organizing the implications by their observable effect: suppression or enhancement of structure formation, internal structure and survival after formation, and emission or scattering by the constituents. We discuss ultralight wave dark matter, thermal and nonthermal warm dark matter, dark acoustic oscillations and dark matter-baryon scattering, elastic and inelastic self-interactions, invisible decays with recoil, primordial black holes, indirect detection, and a possible connection to the LUX-ZEPLIN high-energy recoil candidate. We then ask what new data would discriminate among these models. The existing accelerations are measured outside the tidal radius, where they are blind to the interior mass distribution, to the object's velocity, and to the sub-halo population. We identify four routes that lift these degeneracies: accelerations measured inside the halo, which separate point-mass, cuspy, and cored profiles; lensing and accretion, which test whether the mass is compact; the morphology of annihilation or decay emission, which probes the inner profile; and a search for a comoving stellar population, which tests whether the object is dark and measures its velocity. We also contrast this program with pulsar-timing-array searches for transiting dark matter substructure, which probe vastly lighter objects through a different observable.

</details>


### [106] [The non-equilibrium state of the Galactic disk $-$ new insights from GALAH DR4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25804)
*Peng Yang,Hai-Feng Wang,Joss Bland-Hawthorn,Guo-Jun Cheng,Yu-Zheng Wang,Yang-Ping Luo,Kun Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用GALAH DR4数据，在多维相空间中系统研究银河系盘的非平衡结构（如脊状结构和相螺旋），发现它们与恒星轨道参数、年龄、化学丰度密切相关，支持这些结构具有共同的动力学起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Gaia发射以来，银河系盘的非平衡态引发广泛关注；复杂结构在恒星丰度、年龄和运动学的多参量空间中显现，亟需系统性刻画其在不同相空间中的表现及物理关联。

Method: 基于GALAH DR4中333,412颗具有可靠化学动力学参数的银盘恒星，分析其在化学丰度、相空间（v_i,x_i）、作用-角度（J_i,θ_i）和频率-作用（Ω_i,J_i）等多维空间中的脊状结构与相螺旋特征，并按年龄、金属丰度、α元素丰度及运动群成员身份进行分组对比。

Result: 在R–v_φ平面中脊结构沿近似恒定J_φ分布；J_φ–J_R中脊最显著且与z_max编码强相关，偏好富金属低α星族；太阳邻域z–v_z中清晰呈现相螺旋，不同参量（v_φ/Ω_R/Ω_φ vs v_R）编码呈现单臂或双臂形态；相螺旋在J_R和J_φ投影中仍有残余，表明其携带径向轨道信息；两类结构均具年龄依赖性：脊位置稳定而对比度变化，相螺旋随年龄增大而松散；多个脊对应已知移动星群，剔除其成员后相螺旋信号减弱。

Conclusion: 脊结构、相螺旋与移动星群可在统一相空间动力学框架下理解；结合轨道参数、年龄与化学丰度趋势，表明银河系盘曾受共同扰动，不同恒星成分展现出差异化的化学动力学响应。

Abstract: The non-equilibrium state of the Galactic disk has drawn extensive attention since ESA's \textit{Gaia} launch. Complex structure appears across a multi-parameter space of stellar abundances, ages, and kinematics. Using GALAH DR4, we investigate ridge and phase-spiral structures in chemical-abundance, phase ($v_i,x_i$), action--angle ($J_i,θ_i$), and frequency--action ($Ω_i,J_i$) spaces ($i=R,φ,z$). Our sample includes 333,412 disk stars with reliable chemodynamical data. In the $R$--$v_φ$ plane, ridges trace near-constant $J_φ$ tracks and show coherent variations in $θ_R$ and $θ_φ$. In $J_φ$--$J_R$, ridges are most prominent when encoded by $z_{\rm max}$ and preferentially associate with metal-rich, low-$α$ populations. In the solar neighbourhood ($R=7.2$--$9.2$\,kpc), we recover a clear phase spiral in $z$--$v_z$. Encoded in $v_φ$, $Ω_R$, or $Ω_φ$, one-armed spirals appear, whereas the $v_R$-encoded map shows a two-armed spiral. Projecting actions onto $z$--$v_z$ reveals residual spiral signatures in $J_R$ and $J_φ$, indicating that the phase spiral encodes in-plane orbital information. Both structures depend on age: ridges remain at similar locations with varying contrast, while the phase spiral is more tightly wound in younger and more loosely wound in older populations. Several ridges correspond in $J_φ$ to known moving groups, and excluding stars in all six moving-group regions makes the phase spiral less distinct in mean radial velocity maps. These links support interpreting ridges, phase spirals, and moving groups within a common phase-space dynamical framework. Combined with trends in orbital properties, age, and abundance, these findings suggest a common dynamical origin and distinct chemodynamical responses of a perturbed Galactic disk.

</details>


### [107] [Neutral hydrogen in the Shapley Supercluster core II. Gas content and star formation in the filaments connecting the core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25871)
*Lwandile Gwebushe,Tiziana Venturi,Paola Merluzzi,Giovanni Busarello,Oleg Smirnov,Viviana Casasola,Francesco La Barbera,Mpati Ramatsoku,James Dawson*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用MeerKAT射电望远镜观测Shapley超星系团（z=0.048）中星系的中性氢（HI）含量，结合光学数据，系统比较了星系团核心与外围纤维结构中星系的HI丰度、恒星形成活动、气体耗尽时标和形态特征；发现HI探测率从核心30%增至纤维45%，且纤维中HI富集星系多为晚型星系，气体耗尽时标常超过哈勃时间，表明环境正驱动星系从气体富集向过渡态演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系在不同大尺度环境（如星系团核心与宇宙网纤维）中中性氢含量及演化状态的差异，揭示环境驱动的星系转型机制。

Method: 结合新获取的MeerKAT L波段深场观测（5个纤维指向）与存档的MeerKAT星系团巡天数据（覆盖超星系团核心），并配准Shapley超星系团光学巡天数据，统一分析HI探测率、恒星形成率、HI耗尽时标及形态类型在不同环境中的分布。

Result: HI探测率从星系团中心30%上升至纤维区域45%；纤维中HI探测样本以晚型星系为主，71%过渡区星系和57%气体富集星系的HI耗尽时标超过哈勃时间；而星系团中心HI星系多属过渡区，且HI储量随颜色变红而持续降低。

Conclusion: 纤维结构是星系环境预处理的关键场所，HI气体耗散缓慢，支持‘渐进式’而非快速剥离的环境转型模型；星系在落入致密星系团前已在低密度纤维中启动气体演化。

Abstract: We investigate the neutral atomic hydrogen (HI) content of galaxies across the Shapley Supercluster (SSC) at z = 0.048 by combining new deep MeerKAT L band observations of five filament pointings with archival MeerKAT Galaxy Cluster Legacy Survey observations of the supercluster core. Together with optical data from the Shapley Supercluster Survey, these observations provide a homogeneous view of galaxy evolution from the virialised cluster environment to the surrounding filaments. We compare the HI content, star formation activity, gas depletion timescales, and morphological properties of galaxies relative to the gas fraction and star forming main sequences. The global HI detection fraction increases from 30 per cent in the cluster centre to 45 per cent in the filaments. While HI detected galaxies in the cluster centre are dominated by transition zone systems, the filament population is shared nearly equally between star forming main sequence and transition zone detections, indicating ongoing environmental transformation. Their gas properties also differ: cluster galaxies exhibit progressively depleted HI reservoirs towards the red sequence, whereas filament galaxies remain primarily gas rich. Within the filaments, long HI depletion timescales exceeding the Hubble time are concentrated in specific populations, notably 71 per cent of transition zone and 57 per cent of gas rich detections, indicating the inefficient conversion of atomic gas into stars. The HI detected filament population is dominated by late type galaxies, while the underlying non detected population is primarily composed of early type systems, consistent with environmental pre processing before cluster infall.

</details>


### [108] [Magnolia: a highly magnetized galaxy at redshift $z\simeq9$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25937)
*Evangelia Ntormousi,Andrea Pallottini,Mahsa Kohandel,Fabio Del Sordo*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率辐射磁流体动力学模拟（MAGNA SERRA套件中的Magnolia模型），发现早期星系（z≈9）可在约40 Myr内通过大尺度压缩而非传统发电机机制，快速放大磁场至几微高斯量级，挑战了现有星系磁场演化的理论范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测到高红移（z≈2.6和5.6）星系中强尘埃偏振，暗示存在强、大尺度、相干磁场，与标准星系磁场发电机理论预测的早期星系几乎无磁化相矛盾，亟需从物理机制上重新审视早期磁场增长。

Method: 采用RAMSES-RT代码开展非平衡化学耦合的辐射磁流体动力学（RMHD）宇宙学zoom-in模拟，构建MAGNA SERRA模拟套件；本文聚焦其中Magnolia模型——一个质量约10^11太阳质量的暗物质晕内形成的星系，空间分辨率达~10 pc，质量分辨率达~10^4太阳质量。

Result: 在z≈9时，Magnolia星系恒星质量为数×10^8太阳质量，致密区磁场达几μG、等离子体β≈0.1；磁场在约40 Myr内快速增强；能谱与能量转移函数分析表明，磁场放大源于大尺度压缩及后续从小到大的磁场重组织，而非经典发电机作用。

Conclusion: 强星系尺度磁场可在极高红移（z≈9）迅速建立，表明磁场可能自星系诞生起即起主导作用，需全面修订星系演化与磁场放大理论。

Abstract: The theory of galactic magnetic field growth via dynamo action predicts that the interstellar medium (ISM) of early galaxies would remain virtually unmagnetized on large scales. However, recent observations of strong dust polarization in galaxies at redshifts $z\simeq2.6$ and $z\simeq5.6$ challenge this paradigm. If galaxies indeed host strong, large-scale, coherent magnetic fields at high-$z$, then galaxy evolution and current theories of magnetic field amplification in galaxies should be revisited. To address the full physical complexity of early magnetic field growth in galaxies, we have designed MAGNA SERRA, a suite of radiation-magneto-hydrodynamical (RMHD) cosmological zoom-in simulations with non-equilibrium chemistry. In this first paper from the MAGNA SERRA suite, we present Magnolia, a galaxy hosted in a $\simeq 10^{11}~\mathrm{M}_\odot$ dark matter halo simulated with RAMSES-RT with a $\simeq 10^4~\mathrm{M}_\odot$ mass ($\simeq 10 \,\mathrm{pc}$ spatial) resolution. We find that, at $z\simeq9$, Magnolia has a stellar mass of a few times $10^8~\mathrm{M}_\odot$ and is strongly magnetized, reaching a magnetic field of a few $μG$ and a plasma beta $β\simeq 0.1$ in the densest regions. This strong amplification occurs over a timescale of about 40~Myrs due to the highly dynamical nature of the galaxy, which creates strong compressions and shear. The power spectra of the magnetic and kinetic energies and the spectral energy transfer functions do not suggest dynamo action, but rather an initial magnetic field amplification through a large-scale compression, and a later re-organization of this field from small to large scales. Our findings show that strong galactic magnetization is possible at very high redshifts, which means that magnetic fields can play a dominant role throughout a galaxy's evolutionary history. (abridged)

</details>


### [109] [From a mass invariant in a gravo-turbulent medium to the characteristic mass of the star-forming cores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26162)
*Pierre Dumond,Gilles Chabrier,Jérémy Fensch,Etienne Jaupart*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅基于引力和湍流的机制，通过一个由星际介质密度涨落大尺度归一化决定的质量时间不变量，解释了恒星初始质量函数（IMF）及其前身——原恒星核质量函数（CMF）特征质量在不同星系环境中普遍一致的现象。该不变量与Larson关系标度结合，成功预测了银河系及极端环境（如大质量恒星形成区、早期型巨椭圆星系）中核质量的特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释恒星初始质量函数（IMF）及其前身原恒星核质量函数（CMF）的特征质量为何在广泛不同的星系环境中表现出惊人的一致性（即‘普适性’），是天体物理中的一个基本未解问题。

Method: 基于引力与湍流，构建一个由星际介质（ISM）密度涨落大尺度归一化所决定的质量时间不变量；通过高分辨率数值模拟验证该不变量在自引力湍流介质中的时间守恒性；再建立自洽的自引力多相湍流ISM密度场统计模型，结合Larson关系标度律进行理论推导与预测。

Result: 模型成功预测了银河系典型恒星形成区、高光度大质量恒星形成区以及早期型巨椭圆星系等多种环境中原恒星核特征质量，表明CMF（进而可能IMF）的特征质量起源于湍流的基本普适性质，并在星形成早期由湍流主导阶段及小尺度自引力结构的统计特性共同‘印刻’。

Conclusion: 恒星及原恒星核的特征质量并非由局部物理条件决定，而是由湍流本身的一个普适、基本的不变量所主导，该不变量在星形成早期即已确立，并贯穿于自引力湍流ISM演化全过程。

Abstract: The origin of the observed universality, or, say, the limited variations, of the stellar initial mass function (IMF) and its precursor, the prestellar core mass function (CMF), and thus of the characteristic mass of stars and cores, across a wide range of different galactic environments remains an open, fundamental problem in astrophysics. In this paper, we demonstrate that we can explain this property from gravity and turbulence only, using a mass time-invariant that remains valid for a broad class of turbulent flows throughout the star formation process. This mass invariant is entirely determined by the large-scale normalisation of the field of density fluctuations in the Galactic Interstellar Medium (ISM), induced by turbulence. Using high-resolution numerical simulations, we first verify that the temporal invariance of this quantity is preserved in a self-gravitating turbulent medium. Then, introducing a self-consistent model of the statistical properties of the density field in the self-gravitating multiphase turbulent ISM, we show that the universality of the characteristic mass of star-forming cores is imposed by this invariant together with the scaling of the Larson relations. Our model successfully predicts the characteristic mass of the cores in Milky Way-like star-forming regions, as well as in more extreme environments such as high-mass star-forming regions and massive early-type galaxies. This scenario suggests that the characteristic mass of the CMF, and then probably the characteristic stellar mass, originates from a universal, fundamental property of turbulence and is imprinted both by the very early stages of the star formation process in the gravo-turbulent interstellar medium, when the dynamics of the medium are still dominated by turbulence, and by the statistical properties of small-scale self-gravitating structures.

</details>


### [110] [Disentangling star formation rate indicators in H-ATLAS galaxies: UV, FIR and radio diagnostics from energy-balance SED fitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26260)
*Boitumelo Gaolape,Rhodri Evans*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种恒星形成率（SFR）示踪器（FUV、FIR和1.4 GHz射电辐射）在H-ATLAS样本中的表现，利用CIGALE进行能量平衡SED拟合，评估其一致性、校正效应及红移带来的系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同SFR示踪器在尘埃消光、时标和红移依赖性方面存在差异，需系统比较以评估其可靠性及适用条件。

Method: 对H-ATLAS DR1中Nsamp个星系（中位红移0.12），排除AGN和低质量红移样本后，使用CIGALE代码进行紫外到射电波段的SED拟合，自洽获得消光、尘埃光度和SFR；分析FUV消光与FIR光度、红外超（IRX）的相关性；处理FIRST射电数据的探测与上限；评估红移近似（z=0拟合）引入的系统偏差。

Result: FUV消光与FIR光度强相关（ρ=0.84），更紧密地与红外超相关（ρ=0.96）；未校正FUV SFR在67%星系中偏低超10倍；射电q_IR中位值为2.51，接近标准值2.58；z=0拟合使尘埃温度偏低约(1+z)倍，但对SFR和L_dust影响仅~0.01 dex；10 Myr与100 Myr平均SFR差异<2%。

Conclusion: FUV-SFR必须经消光校正才可靠；FIR和射电SFR总体一致；CIGALE可在普通硬件上高效完成大样本多波段SED建模，但红移处理需谨慎以避免物理参数偏差。

Abstract: We compare three star formation rate (SFR) tracers --- far-ultraviolet (FUV), far-infrared (FIR) and 1.4\,GHz radio emission --- for \Nsamp\ galaxies (median redshift 0.12) from the Herschel Astrophysical Terahertz Large Area Survey Data Release 1 catalogue, excluding quasars, AGN-dominated galaxies and unreliable redshifts. Energy-balance spectral energy distribution fitting with \textsc{cigale} gives their attenuations, dust luminosities and SFRs self-consistently. The FUV attenuation correlates strongly with FIR luminosity (Spearman $ρ=0.84$), largely through their common dependence on redshift (partial $ρ=0.57$ at fixed $z$), and more tightly with the infrared excess ($ρ=0.96$). The uncorrected UV SFR is over ten times below the FIR SFR for 67 per cent of the galaxies, and for all with $A_{\rm FUV}>4$\,mag. For 714 galaxies the FIRST 1.4\,GHz flux density is at the noise level and is treated as an upper limit; as detections, these values push the fits to unrealistically low dust temperatures. The 84 radio detections have a median $q_{\rm IR}$ of 2.51, close to the assumed 2.58. SFRs averaged over 10 and 100\,Myr differ by under 2 per cent, because the model star formation histories are smooth. Without access to high-performance computing, all galaxies were fitted at $z=0$, with distance-dependent quantities rescaled. This lowers dust temperatures by a factor of about $(1+z)$ and biases stellar masses and, at $z>0.2$, attenuations, but changes dust luminosities and SFRs by only a few hundredths of a dex. These fits, with 16.3 million models per galaxy, show what UV-to-radio SED modelling can deliver on modest hardware.

</details>


### [111] [Feature redundancy, redshift, and class-prior effects in SDSS--WISE photometric classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26310)
*Mohammad Sadeghi,Kayoomars Karami*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了星系、类星体和恒星的测光分类性能，系统分离并评估了特征工程、光谱红移、类别先验和样本选择四个关键因素的影响；结果表明光谱红移对分类性能影响最大（贡献80.1%排列重要性），但其物理意义与观测选择效应混杂；缺乏红移时，仅靠颜色等原始测光特征即可取得较高性能；推荐在明确先验下报告纯度/完备率、按红移分段的各类召回率、WISE/红移消融实验及跨巡天验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测光分类性能报告结果差异大，原因在于所用工程特征、光谱红移信息、类别先验及评估样本不一致，亟需解耦这些因素以获得可比、稳健且具物理意义的评估。

Method: 在365万SDSS DR17–WISE源上，训练5种分类器于12种特征组合；采用四方法特征排序、原始特征基线、分组重要性分析、配对自助法，并显式建模先验、星等偏移及WISE/红移消融实验。

Result: 有光谱红移时，调优随机森林宏F1达0.9834；无红移时，10维原始特征达0.9507，24维达0.9551；红移贡献80.1%排列重要性，但导致高红移星系召回率极低（z∈[1.5,2)时仅0.016，无红移时升至0.68）；内部光度红移几乎无增益（±0.0001），外部光度红移仅小幅提升（+0.006~+0.009）；工程特征在有红移时可忽略，无红移时增益全来自颜色。

Conclusion: 光谱红移是强但高度依赖巡天的选择性信号，非普适物理特征；内部光度红移冗余，外部增益有限且条件依赖；建议评估时明确先验、分红移报告召回率、开展WISE/红移消融及独立巡天测试；当前结果受限于SDSS–WISE选源，更深层巡天需重新标定或训练。

Abstract: Photometric classification of galaxies, quasars, and stars is important for future imaging surveys, but reported performance depends on the engineered features, spectroscopic redshift, class priors, and the sample used for evaluation. We separate these four factors on 3.65 million SDSS DR17--WISE sources, train five classifiers on twelve feature scenarios. We combine a four-method feature ranking, raw-only baselines, grouped importance, and a paired bootstrap, with explicit priors, magnitude shifts, and WISE/redshift ablations. With spectroscopic redshift the tuned Random Forest reaches a macro-$F_1$ of $0.9834$; without it, 10 raw features give $0.9507$ and 24 features $0.9551$. Redshift accounts for $80.1\%$ of the permutation importance but mixes physics with selection: GALAXY recall at $1.5\le z<2$ is $0.016$ with redshift and $0.68$ without. An internal photo-$z$ changes macro-$F_1$ by only $\pm0.0001$ (necessarily redundant); an external one adds $+0.006$ to $+0.009$. Engineered features are negligible with redshift; without it, gains come entirely from colours. Redshift is a survey-dependent signal. Internal photo-$z$ is redundant; external gains are modest and conditional. We recommend reporting purity/completeness under explicit priors, per-class recall versus redshift, WISE/redshift ablations, and independent-survey tests. Results are bounded by SDSS--WISE selection; deeper surveys require recalibration or retraining.

</details>


### [112] [Dynamics of spinning supermassive black hole binaries: I. A direct $N$-body study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26348)
*Navonil Saha,Margarita Sobolenko,Peter Berczik,Andreas Just,Walter Dehnen*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过直接N体模拟研究了超大质量黑洞双星（SMBH）在致密恒星环境中自旋对轨道硬化的动力学影响，发现自旋对并合时间、硬化率和能量转移的影响微弱且无系统性，恒星相互作用仍是主导机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究SMBH双星个体及相互自旋构型（通过自旋-轨道与自旋-自旋耦合）对其在致密恒星环境中动力学演化（如硬化的效率、轨道演化、与恒星的能量交换）的影响。

Method: 采用包含最高至2.5PN阶后牛顿修正的直接N体模拟，针对质量分别为3×10⁸ M⊙和8×10⁷ M⊙的SMBH双星，比较七种不同自旋构型（含无自旋基准模型），并分析数值分辨率对恒星获得能量的影响。

Result: 双星并合时间仅变化约1.7%；硬化率与传递给恒星的总能量变化微弱且非单调，无明显依赖于自旋大小或取向；恒星获得能量与双星损失能量（含引力波辐射）守恒误差约1%；约75%的能量由顺行恒星轨道贡献，该现象在无自旋模型中同样存在。

Conclusion: 在所考虑的双星参数与恒星模型下，黑洞自旋对整体并合时标和能量再分配影响有限，恒星散射过程仍是驱动硬化的主导机制。

Abstract: The dynamical evolution of supermassive black hole (SMBH) binaries in dense stellar environments may be influenced by the individual and mutual spin configurations of the black hole through spin-orbit and spin-spin couplings. We investigate the impact of spin on key dynamical properties, including binary hardening, orbital evolution, and energy exchange with the surrounding stellar population of an SMBH binary with masses $m_1=3\cdot10^8\,M_\odot$ and $m_2=8\cdot10^7\,M_\odot$, corresponding to a mass ratio $q\simeq3.75$. We perform direct $N$-body simulations including post-Newtonian (PN) corrections up to 2.5PN order between the black hole-black hole interactions, considering seven spin configurations, including a non-spinning model, and examine the effects of numerical resolution on the energy transferred to the stars. For the adopted initial conditions, the binary merger times vary only by $1.7\%$ of the $\sim3.8$ Myr total inspiral time. The hardening rates and cumulative energy transferred to the stellar population also show modest, non-monotonic variations across the spin configurations, with no clear systematic dependence on spin magnitude or orientation. The energy gained by the stars is consistent with the corresponding energy lost by the binary during the same encounters, including that radiated in gravitational waves, to within approximately $1\%$, which sets the level of the energy error of the balance. We further find that prograde stellar orbits account for roughly three quarters of the cumulative energy gain, a preference that is also present in the non-spinning model and therefore cannot be attributed to black-hole spin. Our results indicate that, for the binary parameters and stellar model considered here, spin has a limited influence on the global merger timescale and overall energy redistribution, while stellar interactions remain central to the hardening process.

</details>


### [113] [Peanut/X-shaped bulges: Returning to the vertical resonance and separatrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26416)
*Sergei A. Rusakov,Viktor D. Zozulia,Natalia Ya. Sotnikova,Anton A. Smirnov*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了N体星系棒的垂直结构和演化，发现BP/X核球的骨架由不稳定figure-of-eight共振轨道的分界线轨道构成；这些分界线轨道随时间下沉并收缩，导致新轨道被捕获进垂直共振，形成轨道聚集，从而塑造BP/X核球最厚部分和X形结构末端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系棒中盒状/花生状/X形（BP/X）核球的垂直结构及其长期演化机制。

Method: 在笛卡尔坐标系和作用-角度空间中分析纯共振轨道，特别关注分界线轨道的动力学行为及其长期演化。

Result: 发现BP/X核球骨架主要由不稳定figure-of-eight共振轨道的分界线轨道构成；分界线随时间下沉收缩，引发轨道捕获与聚集，该聚集对应BP/X核球最厚区域及X形结构末端。

Conclusion: BP/X核球的形成与演化由分界线轨道的动力学主导，其演化最终因势场与相空间几何变化停止而趋于稳定。

Abstract: We investigate the vertical structure and evolution of an $N$-body galactic bar by analyzing pure resonant orbits that form the skeleton of boxy/peanut/X-shaped (BP/X) bulges in Cartesian and action-angle spaces ($θ_\text{res} = θ_z - θ_R, \, J_z$). We show that the main backbone of this skeleton is made up of separatrix orbits associated with unstable figure-of-eight resonant orbits. For the first time, we examine the long-term behavior of the separatrix, showing how it sinks to the bottom of phase space and shrinks. As a result, ``fresh'' orbits involved in a flat bar are captured into vertical resonance, passing through it near the separatrix and creating orbital crowding. This crowding traces the thickest part of the BP/X bulge and outlines the ends of its X-structure rays. The rise of orbits leads to changes in the potential and geometry of phase space in these regions. Over time, when the rise ceases, evolution slows and stops.

</details>


### [114] [Eigenspectra of Poisson-sampled stellar population models: Statistical fingerprints of cool giant stars in unresolved early-type galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26421)
*Anastasia Gvozdenko,Russell J. Smith*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于泊松采样恒星种群模型的主成分分析（PCA）方法，用于从积分光谱中提取老年星系中最亮的冷巨星（如M型巨星）的信息，该方法在红外波段增强冷巨星特征，在光学波段揭示其亚主导贡献，并对年龄和初始质量函数不敏感，但受金属丰度影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 难以从 unresolved 星系的积分光中分离出特定恒星类型（尤其是冷巨星）的贡献，而高分辨率 IFU 观测提供的多谱样本可利用随机采样变差提取额外信息。

Method: 构建泊松采样的恒星种群模型，并对其应用主成分分析（PCA），重点分析 0.45–2.4 μm 波段内不同年龄、金属丰度下的 PCA 主成分结构及其物理对应（如巨星分支形态变化）。

Result: PCA 首主成分在全波段均主导泊松采样变差，对应最亮、最冷的巨星；红外中近似积分光谱，光学中则凸显被掩盖的 M 型巨星贡献；第二主成分与巨星支演化相关；模型对年龄和 IMF 不敏感，但依赖金属丰度。

Conclusion: 该 PCA 方法为 IFU 数据提供纯数据驱动的分析手段，可用于检验早期型星系恒星种群模型中尚不确定的关键物理成分（如巨星支温度结构、金属丰度效应）。

Abstract: The light of unresolved galaxies carries the imprint of their underlying stellar populations, yet isolating contributions from specific stellar types remains challenging. If multiple spectra of the same population are available, e.g. from high-resolution integral field unit (IFU) observations, random sampling variation can be exploited to extract information beyond that carried by the integrated light. We develop a principal component analysis (PCA) approach, applied to Poisson-sampled stellar population models, to probe the most luminous cool giant stars in old galaxies. Across all wavelengths (0.45--2.4\,$μ$m), and over all ages and metallicities explored, Poisson-sampling variation are dominated by a single PCA component. This leading component typically describes the most massive and coolest giant stars in the population. In the infrared, it resembles the integrated-light spectrum since the latter is already dominated by such stars; even so, all features from warmer stars are greatly suppressed. In the optical, by contrast, the method instead reveals cool M-giant contributions which are subdominant in the integrated light. A second PCA component is present in some bands, which is related to the variation in spectrum along the giant branch. Our model predictions are largely insensitive to age or the initial mass function, but show some dependence on metallicity, reflecting changes in the temperature and shape of the giant branch. The method can be applied to IFU observations in a purely data-driven way; comparison to our predictions will provide a new approach to testing some of the uncertain ingredients in stellar population models for early-type galaxies.

</details>


### [115] [On the feasibility of using [Eu/$α$] to identify accreted and in-situ extragalactic globular clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26424)
*Anastasia Gvozdenko,Michael A. Beasley,Katja Fahrion,Søren Larsen,Francesco La Barbera,Alexandre Vazdekis,Ignacio Martín-Navarro*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用28个球状星团（GCs）的高精度积分光谱丰度数据，验证了[Eu/α]比值作为区分原位（in-situ）与外源（ex-situ）恒星种群的有效判据，并将其推广至本星系群其他星系（如M31、M33及矮星系），揭示了GCs的起源与宿主星系质量、金属丰度和空间分布的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证[Eu/α]比值是否可作为本星系群中其他星系（而不仅限于银河系）球状星团原位/外源起源的可靠示踪剂，并探索其在星系考古学中的应用潜力。

Method: 基于28个球状星团（21个河外+7个银河系内）高信噪比、均一处理的积分光谱丰度数据，分析[Eu/Si]与金属丰度[Fe/H]、投影半径等参数的关系；并与M31核球及不同质量矮星系的丰度进行系统比较。

Result: M31金属丰度高（[Fe/H]>−0.7）的GCs具有低[Eu/Si]≈−0.4，属原位形成；低金属丰度GCs则具高[Eu/Si]≈+0.4，多位于大半径处，属吸积产物；M31原位GCs与核球丰度趋势总体一致（除Ba及可能Cr、Ti外）；M33及中等质量矮星系含混合起源GCs，低质量矮星系则以原位GCs为主。

Conclusion: [Eu/α]是识别河外球状星团原位/外源起源的强有力指标；高分辨率积分光谱丰度分析可有效支撑河外星系考古学研究；吸积型GCs主要源自中等质量、中等金属丰度的矮星系祖星系。

Abstract: The chemical abundance ratio [Eu/$α$] has been proposed as a discriminator between ex-situ and in-situ stellar populations in the Milky Way and external galaxies. Here we show with a high-quality, homogeneous dataset of integrated-light abundances for 28 globular clusters (GCs), where 21 are extragalactic and 7 galactic, that this behaviour holds in other Local Group galaxies. In particular, we find metal-rich ([Fe/H] > --0.7) M31 GCs have uniformly low [Eu/Si]$\sim-0.4$, indicating they represent an in-situ population. Lower metallicity GCs in M31 generally show high [Eu/Si] $\sim+0.4$, suggesting they originated in accreted progenitors, and these GCs lie at larger projected radii from the M31 centre. Furthermore, we compare the M31 GC abundances with similar determinations for the M31 bulge for Na, Mg, Si, Ti, Ca, Eu, Cr, Mn, Ba, Ni, and Cu. The in-situ M31 GCs and M31 bulge exhibit similar abundance trends, excepting Ba -- and possibly Cr and Ti, suggesting similar star formation processes. [Eu/Si] in the M31 bulge is consistent with in-situ Milky Way stars, but is higher than the M31 in-situ GCs. For M33 and intermediate-mass dwarf galaxies, we identify a mixture of in-situ and ex-situ GCs, while for lower-mass dwarfs, we identify primarily in-situ GCs. These results suggest that accreted GCs in massive galaxies originated, at least in part, from intermediate-mass, intermediate-metallicity progenitors. We support earlier suggestions that [Eu/$α$] is a powerful in-/ex-situ separator for extragalactic GCs, demonstrating the potential of high-resolution integrated-light abundances for extragalactic archaeology.

</details>


### [116] [The Galaxy Activity, Torus, and Outflow Survey: N. Little Red Dots are not dust deficient: constraints from Seyfert 1 JWST spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26483)
*C. Ramos Almeida,R. Maiolino,I. García-Bernete,A. Alonso-Herrero,L. Zhang,M. Pereira-Santaella,A. Asensio Ramos,S. Hönig,N. Levenson,P. Pérez-González,O. González-Martín,D. Rosario,R. Davies,X. Ji,E. Lopez-Rodriguez,A. Audibert,F. Combes,E. Bellocchi,P. Boorman,A. Bunker,L. Colina,T. Diaz-Santos,F. Donnan,S. García-Burillo,B. García-Lorenzo,L. Hermosa Muñoz,E. Hicks,M. Imanishi,A. Labiano,C. Ricci,D. Rigopoulou,M. Stalevski*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST对近邻赛弗特1型星系的观测，通过光谱堆叠分析，约束了高红移‘小红点’（LRDs）的尘埃辐射特性，发现其并非尘埃匮乏，而是具有中等消光（Av~2 mag）和低再处理效率的尘埃环，从而修正了对其黑洞质量和光度的高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRDs在高红移处表现出X射线和射电弱、似无尘埃等异常性质，若属实将导致其黑洞质量与光度被严重高估；本文旨在检验其是否真为尘埃缺乏天体。

Method: 利用JWST对距离<60 Mpc的赛弗特1型星系进行3–27微米静止帧光谱观测，按Hα或5100Å连续谱光度归一化后堆叠，并与249个z=2.3–9.3的LRDs及一个z=0.1本地LRD的红外谱对比；构建含消光吸积盘与尘埃环的SED模型进行拟合。

Result: 发现仅用本地Sy1标度律可大致再现LRDs红外谱（因子<3），但需Av~2 mag的盘消光与Ct~0.1–0.2的低效尘埃环才能同时拟合光学与红外SED；由此推得尘埃质量约10⁴M⊙，与典型AGN环一致。

Conclusion: LRDs并非尘埃匮乏，其红连续谱主要源于中等消光而非本征高温黑体；此前基于无尘假设的黑洞质量与光度估计应被下调，支持其为标准AGN而非BH-star混合模型。

Abstract: Little Red Dots (LRDs) are compact, red sources discovered by JWST. Most show broad permitted emission lines characteristic of AGN, but other properties differ from local AGN, such as X-ray and radio weakness and an apparent scarcity of dust. If LRDs at z>4 are indeed dust deficient, the bolometric luminosities and black hole (BH) masses measured from SED fitting would be overestimated, and their red optical continuum would be intrinsic rather than reddened. Here we use rest-frame 3-27 micron JWST spectra of a sample of nearby (D<60 Mpc) Seyfert 1 (Sy1) nuclei to place constraints on the dust emission of LRDs. We normalised the individual Sy1 spectra by their broad H$α$ and 5100 A continuum luminosities and stacked them, obtaining scalings that reproduce, within a factor of three, the rest-frame near-infrared emission of a NIRSpec+MIRI stack of 249 LRDs at 2.3<z<9.3 and the near- and mid-infrared emission of a local LRD at z=0.1, with the 5100 A continuum giving the more reliable prediction when modest extinction is considered (Av<1 mag). However, simultaneously reproducing the optical and infrared SEDs of LRDs requires a more reddened accretion disc (Av ~ 2 mag) and a torus with a lower reprocessing efficiency (Ct ~ 0.1-0.2) than inferred for local Sy1 galaxies. This combination offers an alternative to BH-star scenarios that explain the optical continuum as an unreddened blackbody of T ~ 5000 K. Our model, based on local Sy1s, yields a more plausible infrared-to-optical energy balance, suggesting that LRDs are not dust deficient. From the torus model that reproduces the optical and near-infrared SED of the high-redshift LRD stack, we estimate a dust mass of $M_{\rm dust}\simeq10^4\,M_\odot$, consistent with the dust masses typical of AGN tori.

</details>


### [117] [Chemical Cartography Across Time: The Age-Dependent Chemical Structure of the Milky Way with a Homogeneous Sample of 580,000 Giants from Five Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26550)
*Jeff Shen,Neige Frankel,David W. Hogg,Joshua S. Speagle,Shirley Ho*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用约58万颗巨星的统一参数（包括大气参数、元素丰度、距离和年龄），首次揭示银河系盘金属丰度梯度随恒星年龄呈‘U型’变化：年轻和年老恒星梯度平缓，中龄（~5–6 Gyr）最陡；该趋势主要由低α薄盘主导，而高α厚盘梯度始终微弱；[Mg/Fe]梯度则在所有年龄均极弱；结果支持银河系‘由内向外’形成，并伴随气体金属梯度随时间变平及古老恒星盘内充分混合或径向迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过大样本、均匀标定的恒星参数（尤其是年龄和化学丰度），探究银河系盘金属丰度结构如何随时间演化，从而理解银河系的形成与演化历史。

Method: 整合五个主要光谱巡天中约58万颗巨星的数据，采用单一自洽模型统一标定其大气参数、元素丰度、距离和年龄，开展‘时序化学’（chrono-chemical）分析，研究金属丰度梯度（[Fe/H]和[Mg/Fe]）随恒星年龄的演化规律。

Result: 发现盘金属丰度梯度随年龄呈非单调‘U型’变化：最年轻和最年老恒星梯度接近零，中龄（~5–6 Gyr）达最大值（~−0.07 dex/kpc）；该行为主要源于低α薄盘，高α厚盘梯度始终很弱；[Mg/Fe]梯度在所有年龄均极弱（<~0.01 dex/kpc）。

Conclusion: 观测结果支持银河系盘‘由内向外’形成模型，其中恒星形成气体的金属梯度在过去约6 Gyr内持续变平；而最古老恒星近乎零梯度可能反映其形成于已充分混合的盘，或经历了显著的径向迁移，或二者兼有。

Abstract: Mapping the distribution of elements in the Milky Way and understanding how that distribution has changed over time can give us insight into the formation and evolution of our Galaxy. Here we use homogeneously estimated atmospheric parameters, elemental abundances, distances, and ages for ~580,000 giants from five major spectroscopic surveys---all simultaneously placed on a single, self-consistent scale by one model---allowing us, for the first time, to use a large sample of stars with uniform labels and ages that cumulatively span much of the disk to trace how the metallicity structure of the Milky Way depends on stellar age. Our chrono-chemical analysis shows that the radial metallicity gradient of the disk depends non-monotonically on age: it is shallow for the youngest stars, steepening to a maximum of ~-0.07 dex/kpc at intermediate ages (~5-6 Gyr), and flattening to near zero for the oldest stars. This "U-shaped" age dependence is driven largely by the low-alpha (thin-disk) population, with the high-alpha (thick-disk) sequence having weak gradients at all ages. In contrast to [Fe/H], the radial [Mg/Fe] gradient is weak (<~0.01 dex/kpc) at all ages. Together, these trends are consistent with an inside-out formation scenario in which the gradient of the star-forming gas has flattened over at least the last ~6 Gyr, while the near-zero gradient of the oldest stars reflects their formation in a well-mixed disk, efficient radial migration, or both.

</details>


### [118] [Every galaxy is a radio source: a stellar-mass dependent minimum 150 MHz luminosity identified in the LoTSS Deep Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26618)
*Shravya Shenoy,Daniel J. B. Smith,Martin J. Hardcastle,Soumyadeep Das,Marina I. Arnaudova,Kenneth J. Duncan,Luke R. Holden,Huub J. A. Röttgering*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用LOFAR第二版深场巡天数据，结合近红外选源星系的恒星形成率与质量估计，重新检验了150 MHz射电光度与恒星形成率及恒星质量的关系，发现存在一个与恒星形成率无关、但依赖于恒星质量的最小射电光度，并指出其可能源于低水平黑洞吸积（弱AGN），该成分会系统性影响基于射电的恒星形成率测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解低频射电辐射的物理起源，尤其是区分恒星形成与AGN活动对射电辐射的贡献，并评估其对射电SFR示踪器的影响。

Method: 结合LOFAR LoTSS DR2在ELAIS-N1区域的150 MHz深度射电图像与Prospector导出的113,228个近红外选源星系的SFR和恒星质量，分析L_150MHz–SFR–M*三维关系；引入两成分模型（恒星形成+吸积）拟合观测趋势，并估算Eddington比。

Result: 发现质量依赖的最小射电光度（L_150MHz ~ 10^20–10^22 W Hz^-1），尤其在低sSFR星系中显著；该成分与弱AGN吸积一致（λ_Edd ~ 10^-5–10^-4）；两成分模型成功复现观测关系；AGN射电辐射会对SFR估计造成系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 即使在传统认为由恒星形成主导的低射电光度区间，弱AGN活动也普遍存在且连续，因此未来高灵敏度射电巡天（如LOFAR 2.0、SKA）需谨慎建模AGN成分以准确推断恒星形成历史。

Abstract: The second data release of the LOFAR Two-metre Sky Survey (LoTSS) Deep Fields includes the deepest wide-field 150 MHz mosaics in existence, covering the ELAIS-N1 field. Combining these radio observations with star-formation rate (SFR) and stellar mass estimates for 113,228 near-infrared selected galaxies derived using Prospector, we re-examine the relationship between galaxies' 150 MHz luminosities ($L_{150\rm{MHz}}$), SFRs and stellar masses. We not only recover the established radio luminosity-SFR relation and its dependence on stellar mass, but also discover a mass-dependent minimum radio luminosity that is independent of SFR. This behaviour is especially evident in galaxies with low specific SFR (sSFR $\equiv$ SFR/$M_*$), at $L_{150\rm MHz} \sim 10^{20}-10^{22}$ W Hz$^{-1}$, where star-forming galaxies are expected to dominate. The non-stellar emission is consistent with low-level accretion onto supermassive black holes (i.e. weak active galactic nuclei, AGN), spanning effective Eddington-scaled accretion rates $λ_{\rm Edd} \sim 10^{-5}-10^{-4}$, suggesting that radio emission due to accretion is widespread and effectively continuous. A two-component model combining star formation- and accretion-driven emission reproduces the observed trends, demonstrating that AGN activity can bias radio-based SFR estimates. The low radio luminosities probed in this work provide a first glimpse of the populations that will be routinely accessible with future facilities such as LOFAR 2.0 and the Square Kilometre Array.

</details>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Geometric Formulation of Covariant Spin Operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24946)
*Yi-Ning Wang,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于纤维丛和平行移动的新框架，用于处理相对论量子理论中不同速度粒子自旋的协变耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在相对论量子理论中，洛伦兹提升会引发动量依赖的Wigner旋转，使得任意参考系下粒子自旋耦合变得复杂。

Method: 将粒子自旋空间描述为速度流形上的伴随丛纤维，并类比广义相对论引入平行移动，将不同速度下的自旋带到同一参考系中耦合；利用自旋-螺旋度变量构造测地线上的平行移动算符，并由小群协方差唯一确定。

Result: 得到了协变的自旋与轨道角动量算符显式形式，并构建了具有确定自旋和轨道角动量的协变三点振幅基；可构造性推广至高点振幅，并应用于振幅、形状因子及关联函数等的协变分波展开。

Conclusion: 该方法为相对论多体系统中自旋动力学提供了几何化、协变且可计算的统一框架。

Abstract: In relativistic quantum theory, Lorentz boosts induce momentum-dependent Wigner rotations, complicating the coupling of particle spins in arbitrary frames. We describe particle spin spaces as fibers of an associate bundle over the velocity manifold, and different particle's spins residing at distinct fibers. Analogy to general relativity, we introduce parallel transport to bring spins at different velocities into a common frame, where they can be coupled. Using spinor-helicity variables, we construct the parallel transport operator along geodesics and show how it is uniquely determined by little group covariance. This construction gives explicit form of the covariant spin and orbital operators, and yields a covariant 3-point amplitude basis with definite spin and orbital angular momentum. Higher-point amplitudes can be constructively obtained and applied to the covariant partial wave expansion for amplitudes, form factors, and correlation functions, etc.

</details>


### [120] [Hadron masses and pseudoscalar meson form factors in a Poincaré-invariant constituent quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24985)
*Jongheon Baek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个具有庞加莱不变性的组分夸克模型，通过拟合介子和重子质量，并计算介子电磁形状因子，研究了轻介子电荷半径的理论预测与实验偏差，指出超精细压缩效应是导致π介子均方电荷半径偏低的主要原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释轻介子（特别是π和K介子）电荷半径的实验值与理论模型预测之间的偏差，尤其是为何模型给出的π半径偏小、且K半径略大于π半径，这与实验趋势相反。

Method: 采用具有对偶、精细结构及有限程三夸克相互作用的庞加莱不变组分夸克模型；通过20个参数拟合36种介子和27种重子质量；利用光前冲量近似结合Melosh旋转，由质量拟合所得波函数计算10种赝标介子的弹性电磁形状因子。

Conclusion: 该夸克模型虽能较好描述谱学，但在介子电荷半径上暴露其径向结构（尤其超精细相互作用）刻画不足；π介子半径偏低主要源于模型中夸克间超精细压缩过强；K⁺/K⁰半径差异暗示模型对奇异夸克空间分布的描述存在张力。

Abstract: We study a Poincaré-invariant constituent quark model with pair, fine-structure, and finite-range three-quark interactions treated in the diagonalization. A fit of 20 parameters to 36 meson and 27 baryon masses gives an rms deviation of $6.85\,\mathrm{MeV}$, with the common offset fixed by a spectral condition. The same mass-fit wave functions give the elastic form factors of ten pseudoscalar mesons in the light-front impulse approximation with the Melosh rotation. The pion and charged-kaon radii are $0.407$ and $0.411\,\mathrm{fm}$, below the measured values. Unlike experiment, the kaon radius slightly exceeds the pion radius. Replacing the pion radial function by the normalized $S$-wave component of the $ρ$ raises its radius to $0.604\,\mathrm{fm}$, showing that hyperfine compression accounts for most of the deficit in the mean-square charge radius. With the light-quark size fixed by the pion, the $K^+$ and $K^0$ radii require strange-quark sizes that differ by $2.2$ standard deviations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解核子内部结构，特别是其电磁形状因子、横动量空间密度分布及价夸克贡献，需从第一性原理出发的强子结构模型；同时需比较不同但互补的连续谱QCD方法的一致性与物理内涵。

Method: 采用Poincaré协变三体Faddeev方程（彩虹 ladder截断）和夸克+完全相互作用双夸克（q(qq)）Faddeev方程两种连续谱Schwinger函数方法，计算质子与中子的弹性电磁形状因子，并由此推导光前横动量空间（transverse charge/magnetisation densities）；进一步分析并比较两种模型下的束缚态振幅结构（如s波主导性、味对称性与二夸克狄拉克特征关联）。

Result: 两种方法给出相互兼容的横动量密度，符合现代实验参数化；u/d价夸克狄拉克半径几乎相同，但d夸克泡利半径约大10%；d夸克磁活性显著高于u夸克，源于核子振幅中混合对称（轴矢量二夸克）成分；对+x极化核子，正电荷沿+y方向位移，负电荷则相反。

Conclusion: 两种连续谱方法在描述核子结构方面具有一致性与互补性；二夸克关联（尤其轴矢量型）在解释核子自旋与磁结构中起关键作用；d夸克在磁性质上的优势凸显其在核子内部动力学中的特殊地位；结果支持将光前横动量密度作为探测量子色动力学非微扰结构的有效观测量。

Abstract: Nucleon elastic electromagnetic form factors obtained from two complementary continuum Schwinger function approaches -- a Poincaré-covariant three-body Faddeev equation formulated in rainbow-ladder truncation and a quark + fully-interacting-diquark, $q(qq)$, Faddeev equation -- are used to chart light-front transverse charge and magnetisation densities of the proton, the neutron, and their dressed valence quarks. Going beyond a comparison of observables, the bound-state amplitudes that underlie them are also compared. Both amplitudes are dominated by $s$-wave structures; and in the three-body solution, the mixed-antisymmetric (mixed-symmetric) flavour component is dominated by structures with the Dirac character of scalar (axialvector) diquark correlations, so that the association between these flavour components and diquark correlations is realised explicitly. The $q(qq)$ amplitude falls more slowly with the quark-diquark relative momentum than the three-body amplitude does with the corresponding Jacobi momentum. The two descriptions deliver mutually compatible densities, which match expectations based on modern parametrisations of data. Transverse-plane valence $u$- and $d$-quark Dirac radii are practically indistinguishable, whereas the $d$-quark Pauli radius is roughly 10% larger; and the valence $d$ quark is magnetically far more active than the valence $u$ quark, a feature tied to the mixed-symmetric (axialvector-diquark) components of the nucleon amplitude. For a $+\hat{x}$ polarised nucleon, positive charge is displaced in the $+\hat{y}$ direction, with the opposite effect for negative charge.

</details>


### [122] [Theory of Particle Scattering on Superconducting Qubits: From Single Devices to Quantum Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25013)
*Doojin Kim,Kyoungchul Kong,Myeonghun Park*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用超导量子比特作为微波尺度量子靶标，通过虚光子介导的非弹性散射探测eV至keV质量范围暗物质的新方法，强调有限动量响应和集体量子态（如Dicke态）带来的灵敏度增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接探测实验难以覆盖eV至keV质量范围的暗物质，而超导量子比特具有极低的激发阈值（微波量级），可作为新型高灵敏度量子靶标。

Method: 构建了入射粒子与超导量子电路通过交换虚光子发生非弹性散射的一般理论框架，引入有限动量电磁跃迁流和量子比特跃迁形状因子；针对平面Xmon型transmon比特建立真实静电响应模型，并应用于eV尺度玻色暗物质的光子中介散射过程，包含末态玻色刺激效应；进一步分析单比特与多比特（独立操作或Dicke态）的响应行为。

Conclusion: 超导量子比特为探测eV–keV质量暗物质提供了全新、高灵敏、低阈值的量子探测范式，其有限动量响应建模与多体量子态调控是未来低阈值暗物质搜索的关键方向。

Abstract: Superconducting qubits provide microwave-scale transitions and thus offer quantum targets for direct particle scattering with extremely small energy transfer. We develop a general framework for inelastic scattering in which an incident particle exchanges an off-shell photon with a superconducting quantum circuit, inducing a transition from a lower-energy internal state of the circuit to a higher-energy one. The target dependence is encoded in a finite-momentum electromagnetic transition current and, for charge-dominated response, in a qubit transition form factor. A key feature of particle scattering is that a microwave-scale excitation does not necessarily correspond to a long-wavelength interaction, since nonrelativistic particles can transfer spatial momenta much larger than the deposited energy. The full finite-momentum device response is therefore essential. We specialize the formalism to transmon qubits, construct a realistic electrostatic response for a planar Xmon-style transmon, and apply it to photon-mediated scattering of eV-scale bosonic dark matter, including final-state Bose stimulation. We find that the resulting enhancement substantially improves the projected sensitivity at the lowest masses. Owing to the exceptionally low transition threshold of superconducting qubits, this approach opens sensitivity to dark matter in the eV-to-keV mass range, which remains largely unexplored by existing direct-detection experiments. We further show that $N$ independently operated qubits give the expected rate proportional to $N$, while suitably prepared Dicke states can in principle achieve a collective enhancement approaching $N^2$ when spatial coherence is maintained across the array. These results establish superconducting qubits as quantum targets for direct particle scattering and provide a framework for optimizing low-threshold dark-matter searches.

</details>


### [123] [Naturalness scale inference in chiral perturbation theory and chiral logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25062)
*Tara Hassanzadeh,Martin Hoferichter,Lucas Platter,Jacobo Ruiz de Elvira,Matthias R. Schindler*

TL;DR: 本文使用扩展的贝叶斯方法（显式考虑手征对数）推断介子手征微扰论中各观测量的自然性尺度，并以π介子衰变常数和ππ散射阈值/亚阈值参数为例，发现自然性尺度具有强可观测量依赖性，且未必对应于手征展开阶次层次失效时的π介子质量；进一步探讨了格点QCD如何通过π介子质量依赖性精确提取两个O(p⁶)低能常数组合，以替代当前罗伊方程分析中依赖共振饱和估计的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手征微扰论（ChPT）中自然性尺度的选取对理论预测可靠性至关重要，但传统上缺乏系统、可观测量依赖的定量判定方法；尤其在高阶（如O(p⁶)）低能常数需由格点QCD约束时，亟需基于数据驱动的方式确定适用的手征展开有效范围。

Method: 采用扩展的贝叶斯分析方法，显式纳入手征对数项，对π介子衰变常数F_π及ππ散射的阈值与亚阈值参数的手征展开进行拟合与模型比较，从而推断各观测量对应的自然性尺度。

Result: 发现不同观测量（如F_π、ππ散射参数）推断出的自然性尺度差异显著；该尺度并不必然等于手征展开阶次收敛性明显恶化时的π介子质量；为格点QCD提取两个关键O(p⁶)低能常数组合提供了明确的π质量扫描范围建议。

Conclusion: 自然性尺度是观测量依赖的，不能一概而论；贝叶斯方法结合手征对数可提供稳健、数据驱动的尺度判定；该框架为连接格点QCD与手征分析（尤其是Roy方程匹配）提供了更可靠的高阶低能常数约束路径。

Abstract: We infer naturalness scales of chiral perturbation theory in the mesonic sector using Bayesian techniques that are extended to explicitly take into account chiral logarithms. To demonstrate our approach, we consider the chiral expansion of the pion decay constant $F_π$ and the threshold and subthreshold parameters of $ππ$ scattering. We find a strong observable dependence of the inferred naturalness scales, and show that they need not coincide with the pion mass at which the order-by-order hierarchy of the chiral expansion breaks down. As an application, we discuss the requirements for lattice-QCD constraints on the two combinations of $O(p^6)$ low-energy constants that in the current matching of Roy-equation analyses to chiral perturbation theory need to be estimated from resonance saturation, but could be extracted from the pion-mass dependence of $ππ$ scattering parameters.

</details>

*Benoît Assi,Adam Martin*

TL;DR: 本文实现了几何标准模型有效场论（geoSMEFT）在维度八的完整UFO模型，包含全部674个重子数守恒算符，并提供Murphy基表示；模型一致包含维度六贡献与电弱输入参数修正至1/Λ⁴阶，并给出维度八平方项用于EFT截断诊断；开发了可识别任意树图过程所涉算符及其能量标度的独立工具，并生成限制卡以降低事件生成计算成本；几何构造还可推广至任意高阶两点与三点函数计算；所有模型与工具均已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使高维有效场论（EFT）能切实应用于对撞机模拟与唯象分析，需将geoSMEFT完整、一致、可计算地实现为通用事件生成器（如MadGraph）兼容的UFO模型，并解决高维算符带来的计算复杂性与截断不确定性问题。

Method: 基于几何SMEFT框架，系统构建维度八下全部674个baryon-number-conserving算符的UFO模型，采用最小味破坏假设处理费米子算符，并转换至Murphy基；一致纳入维度六贡献及电弱输入参数在1/Λ⁴阶的修正；引入维度八平方项作为截断诊断；开发专用Python工具，自动识别指定散射过程在树图水平涉及的所有算符（含通过参数位移间接贡献者），报告其能量标度并生成UFO限制卡片；利用相同几何结构外推计算高于维度八的两点与三点函数。

Result: 首次公开发布完整维度八geoSMEFT UFO模型（含维度六与1/Λ⁴修正），覆盖全部674个算符并验证于Ward恒等式与独立计算；配套开源工具可高效定位过程相关算符、评估能量依赖、显著降低事件生成开销；几何方法可任意阶延拓至更高维关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作为高精度EFT唯象学研究提供了首个完备、一致、实用且可扩展的维度八计算基础设施，弥合了理论构造与实验模拟之间的关键鸿沟。

Abstract: We present the first complete dimension-eight implementation of the geometric Standard Model Effective Field Theory (geoSMEFT) as UFO models for event generators such as MadGraph. The models contain all 674 baryon-number-conserving operators, with minimal flavour violation for the fermionic operators, and are also provided in the Murphy basis. We include dimension-six contributions and electroweak input-parameter corrections consistently through order $1/Λ^4$, and provide dimension-eight squared contributions as a diagnostic of EFT truncation. We validate the implementation against independent calculations and Ward identities. To make these models practical for collider studies, we develop a standalone tool that identifies all operators contributing to a given process at tree level, including those entering through input-parameter shifts. It reports their expected energy scaling and generates a restriction card to reduce the computational cost of event generation. The same geometric construction allows us to calculate two- and three-point functions beyond dimension eight, to arbitrary order in $1/Λ^2$. The models and the tool are publicly available.

</details>


### [125] [Dirac leptogenesis with charged leptons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25029)
*Juan P. Garcés,Florian Goertz,Maya Hager*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义的Dirac leptogenesis机制，通过引入具有UV起源的高维算符生成标准模型带电轻子质量，并在Froggatt-Nielsen框架下实现，将费米子质量等级与重子不对称性联系起来；该机制依赖于超冷flavon相变，预测flavon质量在1.7–50 GeV区间，并可通过未来轻子味破坏实验间接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Dirac leptogenesis通常依赖超轻Dirac中微子，本文旨在推广该机制，使其适用于标准模型带电轻子，并自然关联费米子质量等级与重子不对称性（BAU）。

Method: 基于Froggatt-Nielsen机制构建模型，引入高维算符生成带电轻子质量；通过超冷flavon相变维持手征不对称性处于非平衡态；结合轻子味破坏约束和sphaleron温度限制确定参数空间。

Result: 证明该广义Dirac leptogenesis机制可行；预测flavon质量范围为1.7–50 GeV；BAU与费米子质量等级统一解释；可被未来轻子味破坏实验间接检验。

Conclusion: Dirac leptogenesis可拓展至带电轻子领域，无需第一类相变或中微子幺正性破缺；flavon物理成为连接味物理与宇宙学重子不对称性的新桥梁。

Abstract: Popular frameworks that explain the baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) are electroweak baryogenesis, requiring a first-order phase transition, and Majorana leptogenesis, which relies on lepton number violation in the neutrino sector. Dirac leptogenesis offers an alternative mechanism that avoids both requirements, instead allowing the BAU to follow from previously generated chiral asymmetries that remain out of equilibrium in the early Universe. Conventional realizations of this scenario typically invoke ultra-light Dirac neutrinos. In this work, we generalize the mechanism to include Standard Model charged leptons by assuming that their mass-generating interactions have a UV origin and arise from higher-dimensional operators. For concreteness, the analysis is performed within a Froggatt-Nielsen setup, which allows us to naturally address fermionic mass hierarchies, too, now linked to the BAU, and we show that the proposed mechanism is viable by supercooling the flavon phase transition. The framework predicts a flavon with a (1.7-50) GeV mass, bounded by the non-observation of lepton flavor violation and the requirement for the Universe to not reheat above sphaleron temperatures, which could be indirectly probed by future lepton flavor violation experiments.

</details>


### [126] [Resonance-aware NLOPS matching for off-shell $t\bar t+tW$ production in association with a Higgs boson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25044)
*Patrick E. Hurley,Tomáš Ježo,Jonas M. Lindert*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的NLO QCD蒙特卡洛生成器bb4lH，用于完整处理包含tt̄H和tWH产生及双轻子衰变的离壳末态，并在POWHEG-RES框架中匹配强子喷注；该生成器统一处理tt̄H与tWH过程，无需图移除或图减法，且支持半轻子事例；同时开发了两个新壳层POWHEG生成器，并通过固定阶结果和窄宽极限验证，结果对LHC tt̄H分析及tt̄H/tWH分离具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一、一致地处理tt̄H和tWH产生过程，避免传统图移除/减法带来的理论不自洽问题，并提高LHC实验中对tt̄H信号提取及tt̄H与tWH区分的精度。

Method: 基于四味方案（含质量b夸克）的pp→bb̄e⁺νₑμ⁻ν̄μH Born过程，采用复质量方案处理顶夸克和W玻色子，完整包含双共振、单共振、非共振拓扑结构及其干涉与自旋关联；在POWHEG-RES框架中实现，结合OpenLoops振幅、allrad多辐射方案和矩阵元基共振投影器；同时构建了两个新壳层POWHEG生成器。

Result: 成功实现了离壳生成器bb4lH及两个新壳层生成器；验证表明其在窄宽极限下与已知固定阶结果一致；NLO/NLOPS预言显示：对包含观测量与壳层描述吻合良好，但在顶夸克线形敏感观测量及tWH富集区域存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 所提方法为tt̄H与tWH联合研究提供了更精确、自洽的理论工具，对LHC当前及未来相关物理分析（如Higgs耦合测量、新物理探针）具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We present a new NLO QCD Monte Carlo generator for the complete off-shell final state encompassing $t\bar tH$ and $tWH$ production with dileptonic decays, matched to parton showers in the resonance-aware POWHEG-RES framework. At Born level the calculation corresponds to the process $pp\to b\bar b\,e^+ν_e\,μ^-\barν_μ\,H$ in the four-flavour scheme with massive $b$ quarks. The complete matrix elements include all double-resonant, single-resonant and non-resonant topologies, all interferences and spin correlations, with both top quarks and $W$ bosons treated in the complex-mass scheme. This approach provides a unified and consistent treatment of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production modes without the need for a diagram-removal or diagram-subtraction prescription. The generator, dubbed $\texttt{bb4lH}$, is implemented in the POWHEG-RES framework with OpenLoops amplitudes, and takes advantage of the multiple-radiation scheme ($\texttt{allrad}$) and of matrix-element-based resonance projectors. Semileptonic events are also supported and can be obtained from dileptonic ones, including QCD radiation in the hadronic $W$-boson decay. In addition, we present two new on-shell POWHEG generators for $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production, the latter being the first of its kind. We validate all generators against known fixed-order results and in the narrow-width limit, and present NLO and NLOPS predictions for the LHC in the dileptonic and semileptonic channels. Tuned comparisons with the corresponding on-shell descriptions show good agreement for inclusive observables, while sizeable differences arise in observables sensitive to the reconstructed top-quark line shape and in $tWH$-enriched regions. These findings are of direct relevance for the LHC analysis of $t\bar{t} H$ events and for the separation of $t\bar{t} H$ and $tWH$ production.

</details>


### [127] [The BSM Rollercoaster, 1980-2000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25060)
*Savas Dimopoulos*

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于1978年至2000年代初超越标准模型（BSM）物理发展的个人历史回顾，以情绪起伏为主线，梳理了两波乐观浪潮及其随后的幻灭，探讨了自然性、超对称、额外维、人择原理与弦景观等思想兴衰背后的原因与实验证据变迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回顾BSM理论发展史中思想兴衰的情绪脉络，理解为何某些理论曾在特定时期极具说服力，又因实验缺失或新证据而失去信心，从而反思‘解释’本身的标准如何随时间演变。

Method: 采用个人历史叙事方式，按时间顺序梳理关键理论节点（如Technicolor、MSSM、LEP数据、大额外维、人择原理、弦景观），结合当时实验进展与学界情绪反应进行分析。

Result: 揭示BSM探索中‘乐观—收缩—再乐观—再失望’的周期性模式，指出自然性原则地位的弱化及人择推理与景观思想的兴起如何重塑理论物理的解释范式。

Conclusion: BSM物理的发展不仅是理论演进过程，更是科学信念随实验证据动态调整的过程；当决定性实验长期缺席，理论判断标准（如自然性）本身亦会发生深刻转变。

Abstract: This contribution is a personal historical retrospective on beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) physics from 1978 to the early 2000s, organized around its emotional rollercoaster. I trace two waves of optimism separated by a multiyear contraction of confidence, both ultimately ending in disappointment when decisive experimental confirmation failed to arrive.
  The story begins in 1978, when the Standard Model emerged as the front-runner. It then moves from the first wave of excitement over naturalness and Technicolor, rumored proton-decay events, and the construction of the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model, to the contraction of confidence in the late 1980s. A dramatic revival came in 1991, when the LEP and SLC precision data pointed toward supersymmetric unification, but it eventually gave way to renewed disappointment over the absence of superpartners and the sharpening of the LEP paradox.
  I also describe how the realization that Newtonian gravity had been tested only down to distances of order centimeters opened the door to large extra dimensions. Finally, I discuss how Weinberg's 1987 anthropic argument for the cosmological constant, the 1998 discovery of cosmic acceleration, and the emergence of the string landscape changed the intellectual status of naturalness itself and led to Split Supersymmetry.
  I have tried to describe not only what was proposed, but why these ideas felt compelling at the time, why confidence rose and fell, and how later evidence changed our sense of what could count as an explanation.

</details>


### [128] [Radiative-recoil corrections at order $α^2 (Z α)^5$ to energy levels of muonium and positronium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25154)
*Gregory S. Adkins,Addison Kovats-Bernat,Elias Mitchell,A. Volkan Turan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了自旋-1/2–自旋-1/2 库仑束缚体系能级在 α²(Zα)⁵ 阶上的辐射-反冲修正，涉及两条交换光子及电子线上两圈辐射修正，结果适用于任意质量比，对改进的μ子素基态超精细分裂和1S-2S跃迁测量具有重要意义。
