Motivation: 为支持即将开展的μ子素基态超精细分裂和1S-2S间隔的高精度实验测量，需提供更高阶的理论修正。

Method: 计算了α²(Zα)⁵阶的辐射-反冲修正，包含两条交换光子及电子线上的两圈辐射修正，并对任意质量比给出精确解析结果。

Result: 得到了适用于所有质量比的精确辐射-反冲修正结果。

Conclusion: 该理论结果显著提升了μ子素能级结构的预测精度，为相关精密实验提供了关键理论支撑。

Abstract: We have calculated radiative-recoil corrections at order $α^2 (Z α)^5$ to the energy levels of spin-1/2--spin-1/2 Coulombic bound systems. The corrections we consider involve two exchange photons with two-loop radiative corrections on the electron line. We obtain precise results for all values of the mass ratio. Our results are relevant for upcoming improved measurements of the muonium ground-state hyperfine splitting and 1S-2S interval.

</details>


### [129] [Small-$x$ helicity evolution with gluon saturation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25401)
*Michael Roa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在大Nc极限下，将胶子饱和效应纳入修正的小-x自旋演化理论中，推导了极化偶极振幅G、G2及其邻近振幅的演化方程；发现饱和显著抑制胶子自旋ΔG的增长（降低约3倍），减缓夸克自旋ΔΣ增长（约2倍），并在小x区域改变其渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解小-x区域核子自旋结构，特别是胶子饱和效应对自旋演化的影响，弥补传统双对数近似忽略饱和的不足。

Method: 在大Nc极限下，将未极化偶极S矩阵（由BK方程给出）引入小-x螺旋度演化框架，推导含饱和修正的极化偶极振幅G、G2等的演化方程，并进行数值求解（结合运行耦合BK方程）与渐近分析。

Result: 饱和使G的增长率由3.66降至2√(1−λ)，G2不增长而振荡；ΔG在EIC能区被压低约3倍，ΔΣ压低约2倍但仍增长；ΔΣ积分收敛的临界α_s由0.156升至0.21；x→10⁻⁶时初值依赖消失。

Conclusion: 胶子饱和显著调控小-x自旋演化动力学：抑制胶子自旋贡献、调制夸克自旋增长、提升自旋积分收敛性，对EIC实验解释至关重要。

Abstract: We include gluon saturation in the revised small-$x$ helicity evolution at large $N_c$. Keeping the unpolarized dipole $S$-matrices, set to one in the double-logarithmic approximation, we derive the evolution equations for the polarized dipole amplitudes $G$, $G_2$ and their neighbor amplitudes. The equations reduce exactly to the known ones for $S=1$ and contain new terms proportional to $1-S$, including one in the equation for $G_2$ arising because the real and virtual contributions that cancel for $S=1$ are weighted differently by the $S$-matrix of the small daughter dipole; this term is ultraviolet finite and acts only for a saturated daughter dipole. Inside the saturation region the growth rate of $G$ drops from $3.66$ to $2\sqrt{1-λ}$, in units of $\sqrt{α_sN_c/2π}$, with $λ$ the growth exponent of the saturation scale, while $G_2$ oscillates without growing. In the asymptotic regime, the gluon helicity $ΔG$ reaches a maximum and decreases, while the quark helicity $ΔΣ$ and $g_1$ keep growing with an exponent set by the dipoles at the edge of the saturation region, obtained in closed form; saturation raises the critical $α_s$ below which the integral of $ΔΣ$ converges from $0.156$ to $0.21$ for $λ=0.25$. For $10^{-2}\ge x\ge10^{-6}$ we solve the equations numerically with a target described by the running-coupling Balitsky-Kovchegov equation, with a saturation scale close to the one extracted from HERA data. At the reach of the Electron-Ion Collider, $x\sim10^{-4}$, and $Q^2=10$ GeV$^2$, saturation reduces $ΔG$ by a factor of about three and $ΔΣ$ by about two; both still grow, more slowly. Comparing three choices of the starting point, $x_0=0.1$ and $0.01$, we find that this dependence is lost toward $x=10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [130] [Helicity amplitudes and angular distributions in $D\to b_1(1235)(\toωπ)\ell^+ν_\ell$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25402)
*Yijia Zeng,Hailong Ma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用螺旋度振幅技术研究了D介子到b1(1235)介子的半轻子衰变过程，并计算了其分支比及轻子味普适性比值，结果与BESIII实验测量基本一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验标准模型和探索新物理需要对重味强子衰变进行理论与实验的精密对比；b1(1235)作为轴矢量介子，在D介子衰变中提供独特的QCD动力学探针，但其实验与理论研究仍较缺乏。

Method: 采用螺旋度振幅方法，结合不同理论模型给出的强子跃迁形状因子，计算D→b1(1235)ℓν（ℓ=e,μ）及后续b1(1235)→ωπ的联合衰变强度、分支比、部分衰变宽度和角分布。

Result: 预测D⁰→b₁⁻ℓ⁺νℓ分支比为(2.6–8.8)×10⁻⁵，多数理论值与BESIII测得的(7.2±1.8^{+0.6}_{-0.8})×10⁻⁵在假设ℬ(b₁→ωπ)=1下一致；不同形状因子模型导致部分宽度和角分布存在明显差异。

Conclusion: 当前理论预测与现有实验数据基本兼容，但需更高统计量的振幅分析以区分不同强子模型，从而提升对非微扰QCD和轻子普适性的检验精度。

Abstract: Using the helicity amplitude technique, we derive the decay intensity for the charmed meson semileptonic decays $D\to b_1(1235)\ell^+ν_\ell, b_1(1235)\toωπ$ with $\ell=e,μ$. Based on the theoretically predicted hadronic transition form factors, the branching fractions for $D^0\to b_1(1235)^-\ell^+ν_\ell$ and their lepton flavor universality ratios are calculated. The predicted branching fractions of the positronic channel span $(2.6$--$8.8)\times10^{-5}$, with most theoretical predictions being consistent with the previous BESIII measurement $(7.2\pm1.8^{+0.6}_{-0.8})\times10^{-5}$ by assuming $\mathcal{B}(b_1(1235)\toωπ)=1$. We further explore the partial decay widths and angular distributions of $D\to b_1(1235)\ell^+ν_\ell, b_1(1235)\toωπ$ and find distinct behavior among various hadronic transition form factors. Further amplitude analysis with larger statistics is thus desired to test these theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [131] [A New Gravitational Wave Power Law from Dissipative Sound Waves in Cosmological Phase Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25581)
*Huai-Ke Guo,Sihao Xiao,Yang Xiao,Jin Min Yang,Yang Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现宇宙学一级相变产生的声波引力波谱在高频区存在一个新的幂律行为，源于声源耗散演化，在强阻尼极限下导致GW谱额外的q^{-2}压制，该特征可用于通过空间干涉仪探测微观等离子体动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示宇宙学引力波谱中幂律行为的物理本质，特别是由宇宙学一级相变产生的声波引力波谱的高频特征。

Method: 理论分析耗散等离子体中声波源的演化，推导引力波谱在强阻尼极限下的高频渐近行为，并研究其与等离子体输运性质和源寿命的关系。

Result: 发现在强阻尼极限下，引力波谱出现额外的q^{-2}压制；该幂律指数对源轮廓不敏感，而转折频率和压制强度可反映等离子体输运性质和源寿命；在弱耦合隐藏扇区等离子体中，转折频率对规范耦合敏感，可落入毫赫兹频段。

Conclusion: 高频引力波谱的耗散特征为利用空间干涉仪探测微观等离子体动力学提供了新途径。

Abstract: Power-law behavior in cosmological gravitational wave (GW) spectra reveals the physical nature of their sources. For sound waves generated by cosmological first-order phase transitions, we identify a new high-frequency power law arising from the dissipative evolution of the acoustic source. In a dissipative plasma, the GW spectrum acquires an additional $q^{-2}$ suppression relative to the perfect-fluid result in the late-time, strong-damping limit. The exponent is largely insensitive to the source profile, while the turnover and suppression strength probe plasma transport and the source lifetime. In particular, the turnover frequency in weakly coupled hidden sector plasmas is sensitive to the underlying gauge coupling and can lie in the millihertz band targeted by space-based interferometers, opening a way to probe microscopic plasma dynamics through the detailed GW spectrum.

</details>


### [132] [Parity restoration in a sterile-neutrino scenario: bounds from atomic parity violation to the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25795)
*Iman Motie,She-Sheng Xue,Alain Blanchard*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超出标准模型的四费米子相互作用场景中，右手中微子参与TeV尺度复合动力学时Z玻色子能量依赖的右手耦合GR(q²)对中性流宇称破坏可观测量的影响，并利用多种实验数据对其进行了约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型的新物理，特别是涉及右手中微子和TeV尺度复合动力学的四费米子相互作用场景下，Z玻色子宇称破坏耦合的行为及其对低能和高能宇称破坏观测量的影响。

Method: 理论推导GR对中性流宇称破坏观测量（如核弱荷、Z极点有效混合角等）的一阶修正；结合原子宇称破坏（铯）、电子散射宇称破坏及Z极点sin²θ_eff测量提取不同动量转移下的GR；利用CMS在Drell-Yan过程中前向-后向不对称性的TeV能区数据直接约束GR；采用运行耦合形式GR=q^{2n}/(q^{2n}+Λ^{2n})拟合并提取宇称恢复能标Λ。

Result: 所有低能数据与GR=0一致；Z极点处GR(m_Z)<1.1×10⁻³（95% CL）；TeV能区GR<0.04（95% CL）；对应n=1,2,3时宇称恢复能标Λ>3.8, 2.1, 1.7 TeV；未来MOLLER、P2和HL-LHC数据将进一步提升限制精度。

Conclusion: 当前实验数据未发现显著的宇称破坏耦合GR偏离零的迹象，对TeV尺度宇称恢复机制给出了严格限制，支持该新物理模型需在更高能标实现宇称对称性恢复。

Abstract: In a four-fermion-interaction scenario beyond the Standard Model (SM), in which right-handed (sterile) neutrinos take part in TeV-scale composite dynamics, the $Z$ boson acquires an energy-dependent right-handed coupling $\GR(q^2)$ that is expected to vanish at low energies and to restore parity symmetry at high energies. We derive the contribution of $\GR$ to neutral-current parity-violating observables. At first order in $\GR$, every low-energy parity-violating coupling is obtained from its SM value by the replacement $\sin^2θ_W\to\sin^2θ_W(1-\GR)$; in particular the nuclear weak charge is shifted by $ΔQ_W=4\sin^2θ_W\,Z\,\GR$, while the $Z$-pole effective mixing angle is shifted by $-\tfrac12(1-2\sin^2θ_W)\GR$. Using atomic parity violation in cesium, parity-violating electron scattering and $Z$-pole determinations of $\sin^2θ_{\rm eff}$, we extract $\GR$ at each momentum transfer. All data are consistent with $\GR=0$; at the $Z$ pole we find $\GR(m_Z)<1.1\times10^{-3}$ (95\% CL). We then use the CMS measurement of the Drell--Yan forward--backward asymmetry at dilepton masses above 170~GeV to constrain the coupling directly at TeV momentum transfer, finding $\GR<0.04$ (95\% CL) for $0.2<\sqrt{q^2}<2$~TeV. Combining both, the scale of parity restoration in a running $\GR=q^{2n}/(q^{2n}+Λ^{2n})$ must satisfy $Λ>3.8$, $2.1$ and $1.7$~TeV for $n=1,2,3$. Future MOLLER, P2 and HL-LHC data will improve these bounds.

</details>


### [133] [Direct simulation Monte Carlo for dark matter production and phase-space evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25812)
*Felix Kahlhoefer,Ann Cathrin Wesener*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）的新方法，用于在相空间层面求解耦合的玻尔兹曼方程，以研究非热平衡下早期宇宙暗物质粒子的产生。该方法无需动量空间离散化或热平均截面近似，能高效处理冻结、衰变、弹性散射和数量改变等过程，并通过双步暗物质产生等例子验证了其灵活性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在研究非热平衡下早期宇宙暗物质产生时，常需求解多个耦合的相空间级玻尔兹曼方程，但受限于动量网格离散化和热平均截面等近似，难以精确刻画复杂动力学过程。

Method: 采用基于随机粒子相互作用的直接模拟蒙特卡洛（DSMC）方法，设计并实现了处理冻结、衰变、弹性散射及暗区数量改变过程的高效算法，完全避免动量空间网格化和热平均截面近似。

Result: 成功模拟了双步暗物质产生导致的双峰相空间分布，并展示了弹性散射与数量改变过程对其后续演化的显著影响；验证了DSMC方法在预测暗物质丰度及其对结构形成影响方面的有效性与灵活性。

Conclusion: DSMC是一种强大且灵活的相空间级玻尔兹曼方程求解工具，适用于广泛非热暗物质模型，有望推动早期宇宙非平衡动力学的高精度研究。

Abstract: To study the early-universe production of dark matter particles beyond thermal equilibrium often requires solving several coupled Boltzmann equations at the phase-space level. We present a new method to address this challenge using Direct Simulation Monte Carlo (DSMC) based on stochastic interactions of an ensemble of particles. We implement and validate efficient algorithms to describe freeze-out, decays, elastic scattering and number-changing processes within the dark sector. These algorithms do not require a discretisation of momentum space on a pre-defined grid and do not rely on approximations such as thermally averaged cross sections. As illustrative example, we consider a scenario with two-step dark matter production, leading to a bimodal phase-space distribution, and consider the subsequent evolution under elastic and number-changing processes. We show that DSMC is a flexible approach to explore out-of-equilibrium processes and solve Boltzmann equations at the phase-space level in order to predict the abundance of dark matter particles and their effect on structure formation.

</details>


### [134] [Resonant Cavity Searches for Millicharged Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25846)
*Miguel González Albert,Ariel Arza,Saarik Kalia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出共振腔实验不仅可以探测轴子或暗光子等超轻暗物质，还能通过TE模式探测超轻毫电荷暗物质（mDM），灵敏度可达ε_m~10^{-19}，在mDM占比极低（f_mDM ≲ 0.2%）时可成为该质量区间最灵敏的探测手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共振腔实验主要针对轴子或暗光子暗物质，本文旨在拓展其应用范围，探索其对超轻毫电荷暗物质（mDM）的探测潜力。

Method: 理论分析mDM在背景磁场中产生的等效电流如何激发圆柱形谐振腔的TE模式，并与轴子DM激发TM模式进行对比；进而估算实验对mDM的探测灵敏度。

Result: 共振腔若能调谐并读出TE模式，可探测到小至ε_m ~ 10^{-19} 的毫电荷；当mDM仅占总暗物质密度的极小部分（f_mDM ≲ 0.2%）时，天体物理限制不适用，此时共振腔成为该质量范围（1–100 μeV）内最灵敏的mDM探测器。

Conclusion: 共振腔实验是探测超轻毫电荷暗物质的一种新颖且极具潜力的方法，尤其在低丰度、特定质量区间具有不可替代的灵敏度优势。

Abstract: A plethora of resonant cavity experiments have been designed to search for ultralight dark matter (DM) candidates, such as axion or dark-photon DM. In this work, we show that these same apparatuses can be sensitive to ultralight millicharged dark matter (mDM) with masses $m_φ\sim1-100\,μ\mathrm{eV}$. Similar to axion DM, in the presence of a background magnetic field, mDM acts as an effective current which can excite the modes of a resonant cavity. Importantly, for a cylindrical cavity, axion DM dominantly couples to the transverse magnetic (TM) modes, whereas mDM dominantly couples to the transverse electric (TE) modes. We project the sensitivity to mDM that resonant cavity experiments can achieve if they are able to tune and readout their TE modes. We find that fractional millicharges as small as $ε_m=e_m/e\sim10^{-19}$ can be probed. If mDM constitutes only a subfraction $f_\mDM \lesssim0.2\%$ of the total DM density, competing astrophysical bounds do not apply. In this case, resonant cavities can be the most sensitive probes of mDM in this mass range.

</details>


### [135] [Majorana dark matter from a thick-to-thin brane junction: Modal stability, real-mediator coannihilation, and a dark-radiation imprint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25872)
*F. A. Brito*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于七维厚-薄膜结的热Majorana暗物质候选者模型，通过壁宽各向异性与四维Majorana质量分裂产生稳定轻态，并利用实标量中介子实现s波湮灭，其遗迹耦合几乎与Majorana质量无关；该模型还预言了可观测的暗辐射信号（ΔNeff ≈ 0.05–0.076），可被下一代CMB实验检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建无需人为引入暗宇称即可自然实现稳定Majorana暗物质的机制，并解释暗辐射信号。

Method: 基于D=7厚-薄膜结模型，利用壁宽各向异性局域化轻费米子，引入四维Majorana质量导致能级分裂；结合模态选择规则和背景软变形松弛，使轻态自动稳定；分析实标量中介子主导的s波湮灭及暗辐射产生。

Result: 获得稳定的热Majorana暗物质候选者，其遗迹丰度耦合几乎与Majorana质量无关；预言ΔNeff在0.05–0.076之间，对应暗物质质量10 GeV–1.5 TeV，与当前CMB数据兼容。

Conclusion: 该膜结模型为Majorana暗物质提供了新机制，兼具理论自然性与可观测性，尤其暗辐射信号有望在下一代CMB实验中被验证。

Abstract: We show that the localized spectrum of a $D=7$ thick-to-thin brane junction can host a thermal Majorana dark-matter candidate. The anisotropy of the wall widths isolates a light junction fermion, which a four-dimensional Majorana mass splits into two nearby states. The factorized transverse profiles impose a modal selection rule on all cubic couplings, and together with the relaxation of the softly deformed background this makes the lighter state stable without an imposed dark parity. Because the localized mediator is a real scalar, identical Majorana particles annihilate in the $s$ wave, and the relic coupling becomes nearly independent of the Majorana mass. The junction sector is a Standard-Model singlet whose light chiral mode is a source of dark radiation. Current CMB data restrict its decoupling temperature and imply $0.05\lesssim\dNeff\lesssim0.076$ for dark-matter masses between $10\GeV$ and $1.5\TeV$, a range accessible to next-generation CMB surveys.

</details>


### [136] [New unintegrated gluon density in proton and nuclei tested with small $x$ processes at modern colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25910)
*Maxim Malyshev,Artem Lipatov,Gennady Lykasov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的横动量依赖（TMD）胶子分布函数，适用于质子和原子核，并通过低能软强子产生、结构函数及高能LHC/HERA实验数据（如重夸克产生、光致光子产生、希格斯玻色子产生）拟合参数；引入几何标度方法推广至轻、重核，并用pPb碰撞中带电强子多重数谱进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为更准确描述质子和原子核中的胶子分布，特别是在不同能量尺度和核环境下，需发展一种新的横动量依赖（TMD）胶子密度函数，并与多类实验数据兼容。

Method: 基于低能区软强子产生和结构函数数据拟合TMD胶子分布参数，并结合LHC和HERA的高能过程（重夸克、光致光子、希格斯产生）进行约束；引入几何标度方法推广至原子核情形；最后用pPb碰撞的带电强子多重数谱检验核TMD分布。

Result: 构建了适用于质子和各类原子核的新TMD胶子分布函数，并成功再现了pPb碰撞中带电强子多重数谱，验证了其在核环境下的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的TMD胶子分布函数具有较广的适用范围，能统一描述从低能软过程到高能硬过程的多种实验现象，尤其在核环境中展现出良好预测能力。

Abstract: We present a new unintegrated, or transverse momentum dependent (TMD) gluon density in proton and nuclei. Parameters of the new function have been obtained from a fit on inclusive soft charged hadron production and structure functions at low scales, and from comparison with data on a number of processes at higher scales results taken at LHC and HERA, such as heavy quarks production, prompt photon photoproduction and Higgs boson production. A modification employing the geometric scaling approach has been made to extend the obtained parton density to the case of light and heavy nuclei. As a test of the nuclear TMD distribution we perform a calculation of the charged hadrons multiplicity spectra in pPb collisions at the LHC energies.

</details>


### [137] [Interpretation of the LZ Event via Inelastic ALP-Dark Photon Transition with Photon Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25931)
*Quanyu Wang,Xiangwei Yin,Tianjun Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的2.6σ显著性的248 keV核反冲事件，该事件难以用标准弹性暗物质散射解释，需引入新物理机制。

Method: 构建ALP通过核电磁流转换为较重暗光子的光子中介非弹性过程（aA → γ'A），推导非相对论晕内与加速ALP的运动学和截面；结合氙同位素、电荷形状因子、探测器效率与能量分辨率，进行三能区泊松拟合。

Result: 晕内暗物质（m≈1.2 TeV, δ≈346 keV）可解释约61%中间能区事件；加速暗物质（v₀=0.05c, Φ₀=10⁵ cm⁻²s⁻¹）仅需m≈5.74 GeV、δ≈6.66 MeV即可解释约62%；入射能量高斯展宽显著影响拟合质量。

Conclusion: 光子中介的ALP-暗光子非弹性散射是一种可行的新机制，能有效解释LZ异常事件；不同暗物质速度分布导致显著不同的参数空间，凸显能谱建模的重要性。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) experiment has reported a nuclear recoil event at $248\pm23$~keV with a global significance of about $2.6σ$. We for the first time explain it via the inelastic process with photon mediator. We consider a dark matter (DM) axion-like particle (ALP) $a$ converts into a heavier dark photon $γ'$ via the nuclear electromagnetic current, and study the photon-mediated endothermic transition $aA\rightarrowγ'A$. We derive the kinematics and cross sections for non-relativistic halo and boosted ALPs. A three-bin Poisson fit to the LZ data includes the xenon isotopes, charge form factors, detector efficiency, and energy resolution. Halo DM, which has mass $m\simeq1.2$~TeV, mass splitting $δ\simeq346$~keV, and near the upper coupling bound, reproduces the event with about $61\%$ in the middle bin. Boosted DM with $v_{0}=0.05\,c$ and $Φ_{0}=10^{5}~\mathrm{cm^{-2}\,s^{-1}}$ requires only $m\simeq5.74$~GeV and $δ\simeq6.66$~MeV to give about $62\%$. We find that a broader Gaussian spread in the incident energy has a significant effect on the fitted masses.

</details>


### [138] [Inclusive and diffractive D meson photoproduction in $pPb$ collisions at the LHC using Pythia8 framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25933)
*Victor P. Goncalves,Luana Santana,Wolfgang Schäfer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Pythia8框架下系统分析了ep和超外围pPb碰撞中的包含与衍射D介子光致产生过程，比较了不同质子和Pomeron部分子分布函数（PDF）下的预测结果与HERA实验H1的测量数据，并给出了LHC上D⁰介子光致产生的快度和横动量分布预测。

Method: 基于Pythia8事件生成器，结合多种质子和Pomeron PDF参数化方案，模拟ep和超外围pPb碰撞中的包含与衍射D介子光致产生过程，并与H1实验数据进行比较。

Conclusion: 衍射过程在D介子光致产生中不可忽略，尤其在特定运动学区域；LHC相关测量有望提升对质子及Pomeron结构的理解。

Abstract: We present a systematic analysis of inclusive and diffractive $D$-meson photoproduction in $ep$ and ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions within the Pythia8 framework. Predictions obtained with different proton and Pomeron PDFs are compared with H1 measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^*$ photoproduction at HERA. We also present predictions for the rapidity and transverse-momentum distributions of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction at the LHC. Our analysis indicates that the diffractive contribution exceeds 15\% at low $p_T$ and forward rapidities, suggesting that diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction could be accessible in future LHC runs. In addition, measurements of inclusive and diffractive $D^0$ photoproduction in ultraperipheral $pPb$ collisions can help constrain the proton and Pomeron PDFs.

</details>


### [139] [Concerning Factorization at Lepton Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26026)
*Patrick Hager,Dominik Schwienbacher*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现轻子对撞机中非全局可观测量中的超前对数项，源于入射轻子电磁分裂产生的有色部分子之间的Glauber相互作用；通过三圈分析确认因子化破缺，并在软截断尺度以下用轻子PDF予以协调；进一步推测即使在纯QED中，软-共线因子化对排他性可观测量也会失效，并在无质量QED中识别出真正的Glauber区域，揭示比预期更丰富的红外结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轻子对撞机中非全局可观测量的红外结构，尤其是因子化是否成立及Glauber效应的作用。

Method: 采用三圈微扰QCD/QED计算，分析Glauber相互作用对因子化的影响，并探讨软-共线因子化在纯QED中的适用性。

Result: 发现并确认了超前对数项及其来源（Glauber交换），指出因子化在非全局/排他性可观测量中破缺，且该现象在纯QED中亦存在。

Conclusion: 轻子对撞机中非全局可观测量具有比传统认知更复杂的红外结构，Glauber效应导致因子化失效，即使在纯QED中也需重新审视软-共线因子化框架。

Abstract: We uncover super-leading logarithms in non-global observables at lepton colliders. They arise from Glauber interactions involving colored partons produced by electromagnetic splittings of the incoming leptons. A three-loop analysis establishes the resulting factorization breaking, which we reconcile with the use of lepton PDFs below the soft veto scale. We conjecture that soft--collinear factorization fails for exclusive observables even in pure QED by identifying a genuine Glauber region in massless QED. This reveals a far richer infrared structure than naively expected.

</details>


### [140] [NNLO QCD corrections to radiative and hadronic $Υ(1S)$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26125)
*Xiangyu Chang,Chao Zeng,Ruichang Niu,Bin Gong,Jian-Xiong Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了Υ(1S)介子辐射衰变和强子衰变的NNLO QCD修正，在色单态框架下取得了与实验数据良好一致的结果，并利用衰变宽度比提取了强耦合常数α_s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高Υ(1S)介子衰变理论预言精度，解决NLO结果与实验偏差问题，并更精确提取强耦合常数α_s。

Method: 在色单态模型和重夸克速度展开领头阶下，完整计算辐射衰变和包含强子衰变的NNLO QCD修正，并分析能谱、宽度及比值的尺度依赖性。

Result: NNLO修正显著改善辐射光子能谱形状，使部分宽度与CLEO数据一致；大幅降低强子衰变宽度，使其与实验相符；宽度比的尺度依赖性明显减弱；由此提取α_s^(5)(M_Z)=0.12135^{+0.0039}_{-0.0036}。

Conclusion: NNLO QCD修正对Υ(1S)衰变至关重要，不仅提升理论精度和与实验一致性，还增强了利用衰变宽度比提取α_s的可靠性。

Abstract: We present the complete next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to radiative and inclusive hadronic $Υ(1S)$ decays in the color-singlet framework at leading order in the heavy-quark velocity expansion. For radiative decay, the NNLO correction reshapes the photon energy spectrum and lowers the partial width in the fiducial window $0.65\leq 2E_γ/M_Υ\leq0.92$, bringing the central prediction into good agreement with reconstructed CLEO data. For inclusive hadronic decay, the NNLO correction provides a large negative shift that lowers the predicted width from its inflated NLO value to a level fully consistent with the experimental determination. Although both individual decay widths exhibit larger renormalization-scale uncertainties at NNLO, their ratio demonstrates a significantly reduced scale dependence due to the cancellation of common short-distance dynamics and the nonperturbative matrix element. Using the window ratio inferred from CLEO measurements, we extract the strong coupling constant $α_s^{(5)}(M_Z) = 0.12135^{+0.0039}_{-0.0036}$, compatible with the world average, and evaluate the sensitivity to uncalculated higher orders via the unexpanded quotient.

</details>


### [141] [Photon-Mediated Axino-Bino Scattering Process for the LZ High-Energy Recoil Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26274)
*Xiangwei Yin,Quanyu Wang,Tianjun Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了光子介导的吸热轴ino-bino散射过程，以解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验观测到的高能反冲事例，并分别在标准晕模型和 boosted 暗物质框架下给出符合数据的参数基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验中观测到的高能核反冲事例，探索非传统暗物质模型（如吸热散射）的可能性。

Method: 计算光子介导的轴ino-bino吸热散射在液氙探测器中的信号，涵盖非相对论性晕暗物质与boosted暗物质情形，考虑天然氙同位素、探测效率与能量分辨率，并采用三区间泊松统计分析拟合数据。

Result: 在标准晕模型下，最佳拟合点为质量m≈506 GeV、质量劈裂δ≈326 keV；boosted情形下为m≈82.87 GeV、δ≈0.626 MeV；更宽的能量分布可允许低至几GeV的质量。两类模型均可合理拟合LZ信号。

Conclusion: 光子介导的吸热轴ino-bino散射是一种可行的新机制，能在不同质量尺度上解释LZ高能事例，拓展了暗物质候选者与探测模型的理论空间。

Abstract: We for the first time investigate the photon-mediated endothermic axino-bino scattering process to explain the LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) high-energy recoil event. We calculate the signals from non-relativistic halo and boosted dark matter (DM), including natural xenon isotopes, detector efficiency, and energy resolution. For a fixed Standard Halo Model (SHM) distribution, a simplified three-bin Poisson analysis yields a best-fit benchmark with mass $m\simeq506\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and mass splitting $δ\simeq326\,\mathrm{keV}$ at the adopted upper coupling bound $g_γ=10^{-3}\,\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$. It predicts approximately one accepted event, with $70\%$ in the middle bin. A boosted benchmark with $m\simeq82.87\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and $δ\simeq0.626\,\mathrm{MeV}$ provides a comparable fit. Broader Gaussian incident energy distributions allow fitted masses down to a few GeV. Thus, both the halo and boosted DM scenarios can fit the LZ signal at different DM mass scales.

</details>


### [142] [Predictions for Inclusive Production Cross Sections of the Higgs Boson at the LHC and HL-LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26281)
*Robin Hayes,Alexander Karlberg,Bernhard Mistlberger,Dermot Moran,Hannah Arnold,Gaetano Barone,Christian Biello,Johannes Blümlein,Valeria Botta,Christian Brønnum-Hansen,Alessandro Calandri,Benjamin Campillo,Alessandra Cappati,Andrea Cardini,Suman Chatterjee,Jiayi Chen,Michal Czakon,Valerio Dao,Goutam Das,Felix Eschment,Silvia Ferrario Ravasio,Giancarlo Ferrera,Alessandro Gavardi,Yanping Huang,Alexander Huss,Stephen Jones,Judith Katzy,Sven Yannick Klein,Matthew Lim,Stefano Manzoni,Josh McFayden,Sven-Olaf Moch,Alexander Mück,Marco Niggetiedt,Mathieu Pellen,Christian T. Preuss,Tom Schellenberger,Lukas Simon,Michael Spira,Lourdes Urda Gómez,Davide Valsecchi,Marco Vitti,Marius Wiesemann,Malgorzata Worek,Sebastian Wuchterl,Silvia Zanoli,Marco Zaro,Licheng Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文更新了LHC希格斯工作组（LHCHWG）关于标准模型希格斯玻色子在7–14 TeV质心能量（含Run-3的13.6 TeV）质子-质子碰撞中各种主要产生模式的包含截面推荐值，质量范围为120–130 GeV；理论不确定性普遍降低，主要源于各通道的高阶微扰计算进展，但PDF不确定性现已成为最精确通道的主导误差源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足LHC Run-3及未来精度需求，需更新希格斯产生截面的理论预测，缩小理论不确定性，并统一处理高阶QCD与EW修正、PDF匹配等关键系统误差。

Method: 综合采用N³LO（重顶夸克极限+精确NNLO顶夸克质量效应）、N³LO结构函数+非可因子化修正、NLO圈诱导gg→ZH、NNLO+NNLL+EW匹配、四/五味方案匹配等前沿微扰计算方法，并逐过程评估N³LO矩阵元与NNLO PDF之间的不匹配不确定性。

Result: 所有主要产生模式（ggF, VBF, VH, ttH, tH, bbH）的中心值与Yellow Report 4一致，但理论不确定性显著降低；PDF不确定性现为VBF和ggF等最精确通道的主导误差；结果已取代YR4及近期临时推荐，成为LHCHWG第五号报告正式推荐值。

Conclusion: 本次更新标志着希格斯截面理论预测进入N³LO主导时代，但PDF与高阶QCD的协同不确定性量化仍是关键挑战；推荐值为LHC希格斯物理的精确测量与新物理探针提供了更可靠的基准。

Abstract: We present updated recommendations of the LHC Higgs Working Group (LHCHWG) for the inclusive production cross sections of a Standard Model Higgs boson in proton--proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies between $7$ and $14$ TeV, including the Run-3 energy of $13.6$ TeV, and for Higgs-boson masses between $120$ and $130$ GeV. All the major production modes are covered. The central values remain consistent with those of Yellow Report 4 (YR4) [arXiv:1610.07922] within the uncertainties quoted there, while the theoretical uncertainties are reduced for most production modes. The improvements rest on specific calculations rather than on a uniform advance: the complete N$^3$LO result in the heavy-top limit together with exact NNLO top-quark mass effects for gluon fusion; the N$^3$LO structure-function calculation supplemented by non-factorisable corrections for vector-boson fusion; the completion of the NLO QCD corrections to the loop-induced $gg\to ZH$ channel for $VH$; NNLO QCD matched to NNLL soft-gluon resummation and combined with NLO electroweak corrections for $t\bar{t}H$, and updated predictions for $tH$; and the matched four- and five-flavour-scheme prediction, with its explicit resummation-scale uncertainty, for $b\bar{b}H$. Since no PDF4LHC recommendation exists beyond NNLO, the mismatch between an N$^3$LO matrix element and NNLO distributions has to be carried as an uncertainty, estimated process by process: it is well determined for vector-boson fusion, whereas for gluon fusion it is doubled relative to YR4. PDFs are now the leading source of uncertainty for the most precisely predicted modes, while residual perturbative uncertainties dominate for the remainder. These results supersede the inclusive cross sections of YR4 and the ad interim recommendations of Ref. [arXiv:2402.09955], and form part of Report 5 of the LHCHWG.

</details>


### [143] [Who Moved My $Z$ Boson? Revealing Resonance Features Inside a Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26291)
*Kevin Alberto Urquía Calderón,Midori Kato,Inar Timiryasov,Oleg Ruchayskiy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在未标注的生成式粒子物理模型ShellFlow中发现了表征Z、J/ψ、Υ等共振态的神经元特征，无需显式监督即可通过干预这些特征调控Z玻色子质量并展宽其不变质量峰，验证了模型内部蕴含可编辑的物理概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索生成式粒子物理模型（ShellFlow）内部是否自发学习到可解释、可干预的物理概念（如共振态），以验证机制可解释性方法在高能物理领域的适用性。

Method: 基于网络梯度构造特征探测器，结合模式识别方法筛选对特定共振态（如Z玻色子）具有选择性的神经元特征，并通过特征激活/屏蔽实现对生成分布中物理量（如质量峰位置与宽度）的定向干预。

Result: 成功识别出表征Z、J/ψ、Υ等共振态的内部特征；可连续调节生成Z峰质量（80–105 GeV）；屏蔽该特征可特异性展宽Z峰而不显著影响其余不变质量谱。

Conclusion: 即使未经显式标注训练，生成式物理模型也能自发形成可解释、可编辑的物理概念表征，为模型驱动的物理发现和可控生成提供了新路径。

Abstract: Methods used in mechanistic interpretability have revealed human-aligned concepts inside language and image models. In this paper, we search for physics concepts inside a neural a generative collider physics model trained on real LHC data (ShellFlow). We discover features inside ShellFlow that represent resonances, such as the $Z$, $J/ψ$, and $Υ$. The network was never told which muons come from a resonance, it was only trained to generate the kinematics of the final-state particles. We use these features to alter aspects of the $Z$ boson: we change its mass between $\sim80$ and $105$ GeV, and removing such a feature from the neural network produces a broader $Z$ peak. In both cases the rest of the invariant mass spectrum remains mostly unchanged. The feature was built from the gradients of the neural network, and its selectivity was built using pattern recognition methods. A generative particle physics model trained on unlabeled data can be modified internally to change a physical quantity.

</details>


### [144] [Towards Consistent Thermal Resummation: Improved Partial Dressing for Precise Electroweak Phase Transition Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26303)
*Dayun Qiu,Fa Peng Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的部分自能修正（IPD）方案，以解决热场论中电弱相变预测中热涨落重求和方法的内在不一致性和发散问题，并在惰性二重态模型中验证了其对相变参数和引力波谱预测的稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热涨落重求和方法存在较大不确定性，亟需一种稳健且系统可控的重求和方案。

Method: 提出改进的部分自能修正（IPD）方案，通过一致引入动量依赖的自能并重组daisy重求和，解决原部分自能修正中的动量重叠问题及间隙方程发散问题。

Result: 在惰性二重态模型中，IPD方案相比其他4种重求和方法给出更可靠的有限温有效势和更稳健的相变参数与引力波谱预测。

Conclusion: IPD方案具有理论自洽性，控制重求和不确定性对可靠预测宇宙学相变及其引力波信号至关重要。

Abstract: Reliable predictions for the electroweak phase transition depend critically on a consistent resummation of thermal fluctuations. However, existing prescriptions often entail sizable uncertainties, motivating the development of a robust and systematically controlled resummation scheme. In this work, we develop an improved partial dressing (IPD) scheme that resolves intrinsic inconsistencies in the original partial dressing prescription, including the overlapping momentum problem and the associated divergences in the gap equations. By consistently incorporating momentum-dependent self-energies and reorganizing the daisy resummation, the IPD scheme provides a more systematic framework for thermal resummation. We perform a comprehensive comparison of 5 different resummation prescriptions in the well-motivated inert doublet model and demonstrate that the choice of resummation scheme can substantially affect the predicted phase transition parameters and the resulting gravitational wave spectra. Notably, the IPD prescription yields a reliable potential and comparatively robust phenomenological predictions, highlighting the importance of a self-consistent treatment of thermal corrections. Our results establish the theoretical consistency of IPD and demonstrate that controlling resummation uncertainties is essential for reliable predictions of cosmological phase transitions and their gravitational wave signatures.

</details>


### [145] [$Zb\bar{b}$ couplings at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26324)
*Radovan Dermisek,Keith Hermanek,Marcos A. Hernandez Ojeda,Taegyu Lee,Navin McGinnis*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用ATLAS的pp→Zh测量结果，以超过9.5倍标准差排除了Z玻色子与b夸克反号耦合的可能性，并指出pp→Zhb过程具有更高灵敏度，未来可提供对Zbb̄耦合最强的间接约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LHC上限制或排除Z玻色子与b夸克的反号耦合极具挑战性，而LEP时代遗留的Zbb̄耦合异常亟需更精确检验。

Method: 基于修改Zbb̄耦合所伴随的新物理接触相互作用项，分析pp→Zh和pp→Zhb过程的实验信号。

Result: ATLAS的pp→Zh测量已以>9.5σ置信度排除反号Zbb̄耦合；pp→Zhb过程具有更强灵敏度；未来数据有望提供最严格的Zbb̄耦合间接约束，并探测LEP遗留异常。

Conclusion: 通过间接方法（利用新物理接触项影响的ZH和ZHB产生过程），可在LHC上高精度检验Zbb̄耦合，显著超越传统直接测量能力。

Abstract: Constraining the $Z$-boson couplings of the $b$ quark, or even ruling out the possibility of an opposite-sign coupling, is considered very challenging at the LHC. Utilizing signatures of new physics resulting from contact operators accompanying the modification of $Zb\bar{b}$ couplings from Standard Model predictions, we show that the ATLAS measurement of $pp\to Zh$ already ruled out the opposite-sign couplings by more than 9.5 standard deviations. We also argue that $pp\to Zhb$, which receives a contribution from the same contact term, would lead to even better sensitivity. Furthermore, we show that with future data this indirect method can provide the strongest constraints on $Zb\bar{b}$ couplings, and can even probe the anomaly in these couplings left over from the LEP era.

</details>


### [146] [Weyl-induced ultrasoft renormalization of the static QCD potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26362)
*Michael Wilson*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了势能非相对论QCD中静态色单态势的曲率依赖超软反常维数，发现Ricci平直背景下的电Weyl张量导致短时色电场关联函数出现t^{-2}项，从而在曲率部分引发α_s^2阶紫外发散；作者计算了补偿软红外极点及有限单圈匹配系数，并验证了物理度规费米子计算与轻味有限项的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用系统在弯曲时空背景下的非微扰行为，特别是曲率效应对静态势及能量标度演化的影响，为QCD在极端引力环境（如夸克胶子等离子体近黑洞边界）中的应用提供理论基础。

Method: 基于势能非相对论QCD（pNRQCD）框架，在Ricci平直弯曲背景中计算超软区色电场关联函数；采用光学维度减除方案处理红外/紫外发散；结合直接物理度规费米子计算与反常维数重整化群分析。

Result: 获得曲率相关超软反常维数ν∂_νc_W = C_F C_A α_s^2/(3π)；确认α_s^2阶曲率紫外发散比平直空间早两阶；验证软红外极点与超软常数在曲率重标度下的抵消机制；确定静态能量中存在曲率依赖的α_s^2 ln α_s项。

Conclusion: 曲率显著改变pNRQCD中超软动力学的标度行为，其效应在α_s^2阶即显现且主导对数修正，表明广义相对论与QCD在强耦合低能区存在不可忽略的协同演化。

Abstract: We derive the leading curvature-dependent ultrasoft anomalous dimension of the static color-singlet potential in potential nonrelativistic QCD. In a Ricci-flat background the electric Weyl tensor introduces a $t^{-2}$ term in the short-time chromoelectric correlator. The resulting dipole self-energy is ultraviolet divergent at order $α_s^2$ in the curvature sector, two orders earlier than the flat-space contribution. For $W=C_{μανβ}r^μu^αr^νu^β$ and $V_s^{(W)}=c_W W/r$, we obtain $ν\,\partial_νc_W=C_F C_Aα_s^2/(3π)$. We calculate the compensating soft infrared pole and the finite one-loop matching coefficient in an optical dimensional subtraction scheme. A direct physical-metric fermion calculation reproduces the finite light-flavor term. We also exhibit the cancellation of the leading regulated infrared overlap and the compensating transformation of soft and ultrasoft constants under an evanescent curvature rescaling. In the perturbative adiabatic regime the anomalous dimension fixes the coefficient of a curvature-dependent $α_s^2\lnα_s$ term in the static energy.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum Autoencoder on Real Hardware for New Physics Searches at Muon Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26364)
*Shuai Zhang,Ji-Chong Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用六量子比特的量子自编码器，在天眼176量子计算机上对μ⁺μ⁻→γγγ过程中标准模型与反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs）事件进行重建损失测量，成功区分新物理信号与标准模型背景，并给出了aQGCs系数的预期约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缪子对撞机上探测三光子过程中的新物理信号，尤其是反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs），需更高效的方法以区分微弱信号与标准模型背景。

Method: 采用六量子比特量子自编码器，在Tianyan176量子计算机上实现硬件级训练与推断，通过比较SM与aQGCs事件的重建损失来判别新物理信号。

Result: 量子自编码器在硬件上成功实现，能有效区分aQGCs信号与SM背景；同时导出了aQGCs耦合系数的预期实验约束，并与传统方法结果进行了对比。

Conclusion: 量子自编码器为高能物理中新物理搜索提供了一种可行且有潜力的量子机器学习方案，尤其适用于当前含噪声中等规模量子设备。

Abstract: We apply a quantum autoencoder to search for new physics (NP) in the tri-photon process $μ^+μ^-\toγγγ$ at muon colliders. Using a six-qubit circuit on the Tianyan176 quantum computer, we measure the reconstruction losses for Standard Model (SM) events and anomalous quartic gauge couplings (aQGCs) events. The results demonstrate that the hardware-implemented quantum autoencoder effectively discriminates NP signals from the SM background. Concurrently, we derive the expected constraints on the aQGCs coefficients and compare them with those obtained from conventional methods.

</details>


### [148] [Constraint on anomalous $W^-W^+γ$ coupling in $\ell^\pm γ\slashed{E}$ final state at NLO QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26392)
*Amir Subba,Yu Shi,Athul P,Ritesh K Singh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了13 TeV质子-质子对撞中通过ℓ±γ⧸E末态探测W⁻W⁺γ异常三重规范耦合，利用W玻色子的8个极化不对称性及m_{Wγ}≥1 TeV分布，在NLO QCD精度下给出CP偶和CP奇耦合的严格限制，发现系统误差主导灵敏度，且CP奇耦合结果远弱于电子电偶极矩实验限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高对W⁻W⁺γ异常三重规范耦合（特别是CP破坏项）的实验约束能力，并评估高亮度LHC下该通道的物理潜力与系统不确定性影响。

Method: 在√s=13 TeV下，基于pp→ℓ±γ⧸E过程，采用NLO QCD修正计算；构造8个W玻色子极化不对称性，按Δη_{ℓγ}划分为4个区域，并结合m_{Wγ}≥1 TeV高质量分布；进行四参数马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）联合拟合以获取边缘化限制。

Result: CP奇耦合\widetilde{λ}_γ的限制显著优于CMS WW+WZ组合结果，λ_γ限制具竞争力；但提升信噪比受限于系统误差，统计亮度增加带来的改进甚微；电子EDM实验对两个CP奇耦合的限制比本工作强约2.5个数量级。

Conclusion: ℓ±γ⧸E道在NLO精度下是约束W⁻W⁺γ CP奇耦合的有效探针，但其灵敏度已系统受限；未来需降低系统不确定性或借助更敏感观测量（如EDM）协同检验新物理。

Abstract: We study the anomalous $W^-W^+γ$ triple gauge couplings in the $pp\to \ell^\pmγ\slashed{E}$ channel at $\sqrt{s}=13$~TeV with next-to-leading order QCD corrections. Eight polarization asymmetries of the reconstructed $W$ boson, measured in four disjoint regions of $Δη_{\ellγ}$ and combined with the $m_{Wγ} \ge 1$ TeV distribution, are used to constrain the two CP-even and two CP-odd couplings. The bounds improve only marginally with increasing luminosity, far below the statistical expectation, while they degrade substantially when the systematic uncertainties are relaxed, showing that the sensitivity is systematically limited. A simultaneous four-parameter MCMC fit is performed to obtain marginalized limits. Relative to the CMS $WW+WZ$ combination, our bounds are significantly tighter for the CP-odd coupling $\widetildeλ_γ$ and competitive for $λ_γ$. The CP-odd results are also compared with the electron EDM limit, which constrains both couplings by roughly $2.5$ orders of magnitude beyond the collider reach.

</details>


### [149] [Thermal Resummation Effects on Electroweak Phase Transitions and Gravitational Waves in Conformal and Non-Conformal 2HDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26417)
*Yu-Bo Zhao,Ji-Wei Li,Zhi-Wei Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文量化了热重求和对经典共形与非共形双Higgs二重态模型中电弱相变及引力波预测的影响，比较了多场部分修饰（PD）、Parwani和Arnold-Espinosa（AE）三种热场论处理方法，发现PD通常给出最强、最过冷的相变，并带来最大的引力波峰值振幅，且热重求和是相关预测中的主要不确定性来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热重求和方法的选择对电弱相变动力学和引力波信号预测具有显著影响，但其不确定性尚未被系统量化，尤其在多标量场模型中。

Method: 采用多场部分修饰（PD）方法（保留完整热函数并由gap方程确定标量质量与混合），并与Parwani和Arnold-Espinosa（AE）方法在相同零温输入下进行对比；分析相变强度（Tp/Tc）、完成方式、引力波峰值振幅与频率，并考察PD中路径依赖性带来的内在不确定性。

Result: 在共形模型中，PD普遍导致更强、更过冷的相变（Tp/Tc ≈ 0.5），引力波峰值振幅最大；非共形模型中无统一排序，不同方法可改变热演化类型；PD与AE的引力波峰值振幅差异可达9个数量级，频率差异约50倍；PD路径依赖引起的参数变化（如Tp、振幅）远小于方法间差异。

Conclusion: 热重求和是电弱相变与引力波预测中不可忽略的关键不确定性来源，多场PD虽更自洽但存在路径依赖，而不同热处理方法间的系统性差异远超其内在模糊性，亟需更精确的热场论框架。

Abstract: We quantify how thermal resummation affects electroweak phase transitions and gravitational-wave predictions in classically conformal and non-conformal two-Higgs-doublet models. We compare multi-field partial dressing (PD), which retains the full thermal functions and determines scalar masses and mixing from gap equations, with the Parwani and Arnold-Espinosa (AE) prescriptions at identical zero-temperature inputs. Completed first-order transitions dominate the conformal scan under all three prescriptions. PD typically gives the strongest and most supercooled transitions, reaching $T_p/T_c \simeq 0.5$ versus $T_p/T_c \gtrsim 0.8$ for Parwani and AE. At every conformal point where all three predict completion, PD gives the largest gravitational-wave peak amplitude, followed by Parwani and AE. The non-conformal model has no universal ordering, and the prescription changes the type of thermal history across extended regions of parameter space. For representative benchmarks, PD and AE differ by up to about nine orders of magnitude in peak amplitude and a factor of about fifty in peak frequency. In multi-field PD, the dressed tadpole is not a gradient, so the reconstructed potential depends on the integration path. In benchmark tests, changing the path shifts the transition temperatures by at most about 7% and the peak amplitude by up to a factor of about three. This intrinsic ambiguity is smaller than the largest differences between prescriptions. Thermal resummation is a major source of uncertainty in these phase-transition and gravitational-wave predictions.

</details>


### [150] [Future avenues for precision oscillation measurements with compact movable reactors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26418)
*Matheus Hostert,Stephan A. Meighen-Berger,Maxim Pospelov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用可移动反应堆中微子源与江门中微子实验（JUNO）协同测量θ₁₃，通过双基线观测抑制系统误差，有望将θ₁₃相对精度提升至2.5%–4%，显著超越当前水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有及规划中的中微子实验难以超越大亚湾对θ₁₃的测量精度；需创新策略以压制统计与系统误差。

Method: 设计两种可移动反应堆方案（地下~20 MWth微堆与地表~400 MWth反应堆），配合JUNO在两个不同基线处测量反中微子通量，并用近端监测器标定通量，结合Daya Bay同位素能谱协方差建模HALEU/LEU堆芯，量化系统误差影响。

Result: 地下微堆可在十年内将θ₁₃相对误差降至约4%（较JUNO单独提升3–4倍）；地表反应堆若通量监测精度达亚百分比级，约三年可达当前全球拟合精度2.5%。

Conclusion: 可移动反应堆+JUNO双基线方案是突破θ₁₃测量精度瓶颈的有效新路径，对中微子振荡参数精确测定具有重要前景。

Abstract: No planned neutrino experiment is expected to surpass Daya Bay's precision on $\thone$. Doing so requires innovative strategies to reduce statistical and systematic uncertainties. In this study, we consider a futuristic program with a movable reactor antineutrino source placed within short distances ($L\lesssim 3\,\km$) from the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) detector. Due to the large fiducial mass of JUNO, even a relatively small reactor can produce a statistically significant number of events over a few years to probe the $\lesssim 8.7\% \,\nuebar$ disappearance set by $\thone$. We consider two possibilities: a $\mathcal{O}(20\mwth)$ microreactor in alcoves of the access tunnel to the underground laboratory, and a $\mathcal{O}(400 \mwth)$ unit on the surface. In both cases the reactor is observed by a small flux monitor detector near the source, while JUNO measures the antineutrino flux at two baselines, suppressing correlated flux and detector systematics. Using the measured Daya Bay per-isotope spectra and its covariance, we model a high-assay low-enriched uranium (HALEU) core for the microreactor and a low-enriched uranium (LEU) core for the surface unit, and discuss various potential sources of systematic uncertainties and their impact on the precision of $\thone$. The underground microreactor could improve on JUNO's reach by a factor of three to four, to $\relsth \simeq 4\%$ over a decade, while the surface reactor would reach the present global-fit precision, $\relsth=2.5\%$, in about three years, provided the emission rate is monitored across reactor relocations to the sub-percent level.

</details>


### [151] [$Zγ$ production in gluon fusion at NLO+NLL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26520)
*Chinmoy Dey,Niraj Koirala,M. C. Kumar,Vaibhav Pandey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在13.6 TeV LHC上通过胶子融合产生Zγ过程的NLO QCD修正及NLL阈值重求和效应，发现NLO修正使LO截面增强约83%，并减小尺度不确定性；NLL重求和进一步提升NLO截面约23%，并进一步压低高不变质量区的尺度不确定性，同时评估了αs、PDF及顶夸克贡献的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确预测Zγ产生截面对于检验标准模型和新物理信号至关重要，而胶子融合道在高能对撞机中不可忽略，但其QCD辐射效应显著，需高阶修正和阈值重求和以提高理论精度。

Method: 采用NLO QCD固定阶计算结合NLL阈值重求和，并匹配至NLO结果；使用七点尺度变化评估理论不确定性；纳入强耦合常数αs和PDF不确定性分析；利用Born Improved Theory（BIT）估算顶夸克贡献。

Result: NLO修正使LO截面提升约83%，尺度不确定性从22.3%降至15.2%；NLL+NLO组合使截面再增约23%，在Q=1000 GeV处尺度不确定性由25%降至约16%；给出了不同LHC质心能量下的总截面及Q≤1000 GeV的不变质量分布；量化了αs、PDF及顶夸克效应对结果的影响。

Conclusion: NLO+ NLL联合计算显著提升了Zγ胶子融合过程的理论预测精度，尤其在高不变质量区域，为LHC实验提供更可靠的基准预测，并凸显了高阶QCD效应与非微扰输入不确定性的重要性。

Abstract: We study the production of $Zγ$ in gluon fusion at hadron colliders at the next-to-leading-order (NLO) in QCD. At the $13.6$ TeV LHC, we find that these NLO corrections are found to enhance the leading-order (LO) cross section by about $83$\% and reduce the conventional seven-point scale uncertainty from $22.3$\% at LO to $15.2$\% at NLO. We further perform the threshold resummation at next-to-leading-logarithmic (NLL) accuracy and match to the fixed order NLO results. We have presented the production cross section for various center of mass energies at the LHC. We find that this NLL resummation enhances the NLO production cross section by about $23$\%. We also give the invariant mass distribution up to $Q=1000$ GeV for $13.6$ TeV LHC. In this case, the resummation reduces the seven-point scale uncertainty from $25\%$ at NLO to about $16$\% at NLO+NLL for $Q=1000$ GeV. We also present the uncertainties due to the strong coupling constant and the parton distribution functions in our results. We further estimate the contribution of the top quark in our predictions, using the Born Improved Theory (BIT).

</details>


### [152] [Self-gravity depletes superradiant clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26541)
*Mateja Bošković,Lucas Gathercole,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Samuel J. Witte*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出超辐射云中的超轻标量场不能简单地被建模为自由场，其自引力效应会重新分配粒子能级，显著削弱云的质量和相关信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超辐射云通常被简化为自由场模型，但实际中自引力和环境效应（如伴星）可能起主导作用，需重新评估其物理行为。

Method: 通过比较自引力与轴子自相互作用的相对强度，并分析其对能级粒子分布的影响，结合二元伴星的补偿效应进行理论建模。

Result: 自引力在衰变常数较大时主导粒子再分布，导致云质量大幅下降、信号减弱；伴星可部分抵消该效应。

Conclusion: 任何对超辐射信号的评估都必须同时考虑自引力和周围环境（如伴星），自由场近似在现实中不成立。

Abstract: Superradiant clouds of ultralight scalars around black holes are often modeled as free fields, whose levels grow independently and decay slowly through gravitational-wave emission. We show that this limit is never realized. The cloud's self-gravity redistributes particles between levels, just as in the case of axion self-interactions, and dominates over self-interactions for decay constants $f_a\gtrsim6\times10^{16}\,{\rm GeV}\,(α/0.1)$, with $α$ the gravitational fine-structure constant. Self-gravity depletes the cloud by orders of magnitude and feeds more extended levels, weakening all signals that rely on the existence of a large cloud mass. A binary companion, however, can partly offset the depletion. Any assessment of superradiant signals must therefore account for both self-gravity and the cloud's environment.

</details>


### [153] [Refractive Neutrino Mass: Effects of Fluctuations of the Dark Matter Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26549)
*Soham Sahasrabuddhe,Manibrata Sen,Alexei Y. Smirnov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了超轻标量暗物质（ULDM）通过折射效应影响中微子振荡的可能性，模拟了不同质量与自相互作用强度的复/实标量场对JUNO和T2K实验中微子振荡概率的影响，并分析了多种物理机制（如多场、引力扰动、中微子背景相互作用）对约束的缓解作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的中微子振荡现象，探索其是否可由超轻标量暗物质引起的折射性中微子质量平方项来实现，并检验该模型在当前实验下的可行性。

Method: 通过数值模拟含自相互作用的标量场干涉图样，结合中微子传播方程，计算JUNO和T2K实验中的振荡概率；区分复标量场与实标量场情形，并考虑太阳/地球引力、轴子宇宙、遗迹中微子背景等修正效应。

Result: 复标量场无自耦合时，m_φ ≲ 10⁻¹⁶ eV 可行；有排斥自相互作用时，m_φ ≳ 10⁻¹⁶ eV 下偏差ΔP ≈ 0.07；实标量场在10⁻²²–10⁻¹⁰ eV区间因康普顿振荡显著扭曲概率，仅当m_φ ≳ 10⁻⁹ eV时可忽略；多场、引力及中微子背景等效应可放宽质量约束。

Conclusion: ULDM诱导的折射质量机制仍具可行性，但其参数空间受标量场性质（实/复、自耦合）及天体环境显著影响；需结合多实验与多物理效应综合检验。

Abstract: Observed neutrino oscillations can be reproduced by a refractive neutrino mass-squared arising from interactions with ultralight scalar dark matter (ULDM). The wave behaviour of the scalar field gives rise to a fluctuating interference pattern, inducing spatial and temporal variations in the refractive mass that can distort neutrino oscillation probabilities. We simulate the interference patterns of the scalar field in the presence of self-interactions. Using results of these simulations, the oscillation probabilities for JUNO and T2K experiments are computed. We find that the refractive mass scenario remains viable for the complex scalar field without self-coupling for $m_φ\lesssim 10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$. For $m_φ\gtrsim10^{-16}~\mathrm{eV}$, repulsive self-interactions narrow the density distribution, reducing the deviation of the survival probability at the first minimum to $ΔP \approx 0.07$ for an appropriate self-interaction coupling. In contrast, for a real scalar field, rapid oscillations with the Compton frequency distort the probabilities for scalar masses $ 10^{-22}~\mathrm{eV} \lesssim m_φ\lesssim 10^{-10} ~\mathrm{eV}$, and self-interactions do not remove this distortion. This distortion becomes negligible only for $m_φ\gtrsim10^{-9}~\mathrm{eV}$. We explore additional effects which could modify the interference pattern and relax the constraints on the required values of $m_φ$. These include scenarios with multiple scalar fields (the axiverse), the gravitational effects of the Sun and Earth on the granule structure, and interactions with the relic neutrino background.

</details>
